- **2026-07-02 — Fix: CSV import — blank-column immunity + content-aware detection for any bank (this PR).** Reza uploaded a real AMEX/Qantas CSV that still failed (now with the CLEAR message from the prior fix: "Missing or invalid amount") and asked how to manage different bank formats. Reproduced (§19.2): the CSV had a **blank column**, and both `detectBankMapping` + `findColumnIndex` matched via `p.includes(h)` — where `p.includes('')` is always true — so the empty header matched EVERY pattern → "CBA Transaction Export" matched 100% and every column lookup collapsed onto the blank column → amount read from an empty cell. Fix: shared `headerMatches(h,p)` (empty header never matches; reverse-includes gated to ≥3 chars) used by both detectors; and `inferColumns` (data-driven date/amount/description) now runs for ANY headed CSV where no known bank matches, not just headerless. Verified the real file: 20 rows → 20 normalised, 0 errors (amount +$500 IN, desc "BPAY PAYMENT"). +2 tests (131 bank/neobrain green; real file kept out of the repo); `tsc` + `neomatrix:check` green; §20.4 **10/10**.
- **2026-07-02 — Fix: CSV import 500 — headerless CSV support + crash guard (PR #1328, merged).** After #1327, Reza's CSV still failed: `POST /api/accounts/new/import` → 500 "Failed to process import". REPRODUCED locally (Vercel log API was timing out): a **headerless** CSV (NAB + several AU banks) made `parseCSV` eat the first row as a phantom header; the rest failed column detection → `normaliseTransactions` dropped all rows → the route (which only checked the raw parsed count) computed `dateRange = new Date(Math.min(...[]))` = Invalid Date → Prisma 500. Fix: (1) `rowLooksLikeData` header auto-detect + `inferColumns()` — infer date/amount/description from the DATA when headerless (amount inference prefers negatives + smallest magnitude so a running balance column is never mistaken for the amount, §19); (2) route guard → clear 400 naming the reason when 0 rows survive normalisation, instead of the Invalid-Date crash. +3 headerless tests (129 bank/neobrain green); Neomatrix import-route anchors updated (278/285/369, §21.2.1); `neomatrix:check` green; §20.4 **10/10**. Honest residual: couldn't read Reza's exact file/prod logs, so an unusual bank layout now returns a named 400 instead of a blind 500.
- **2026-07-02 — Fix: CSV import — account import route now accepts CSV, not just QIF (PR #1327, merged).** Reza: "some banks still don't have QIF! … fix CSV upload." The dialog offered "QIF or CSV" and the picker accepted `.csv`, but `POST /api/accounts/[id]/import` only handled QIF → "Unsupported file format." Wired the EXISTING `parseCSV` (`lib/bank/parsers/csv.ts`, same `ParsedFile` shape) into the route (`.csv` → parseCSV; QIF stays content-sniffed). Also fixed a real parser data-drop I found while testing (§19): the greedy 60%-header bank-mapping fuzzy-match could pick a bank (e.g. ANZ) for a generic `date,description,amount` CSV, not find its differently-named column, and silently drop the description → blank merchants. Added `resolveCol()` — a mapping column name that isn't in the actual headers falls through to the positional default (date→0, description→1) instead of -1; optional columns stay -1. Real banks (NAB/CBA/Westpac/ING/Up, distinctive headers) unaffected. +4 CSV parser tests (126 bank/neobrain green); Neomatrix import-route anchors updated (§21.2.1); `neomatrix:check` green; §20.5 **10/10**.

No suspected-issue found; locks the income-tax bracket-boundary P0 fix + the SG-cap P1 fix against regression. Governance shipped alongside: CLAUDE.md **Part 20** (3×/10-10 self-review before sign-off) + **§20.4** (any financial build = recorded 10/10) + **Part 21** (every build runs through CLAUDE.md + Neomatrix). **Pending:** A1 SMSF/CGT + health/CFO/intelligence/reports; N2 2D explorer; N5 3D. See `PHASE_53_MONITRAX_NEOMATRIX.md` §0 + `0·NEOMATRIX` workstream. All N0–A1-tax increments merged + prod-verified (each prod deploy READY with the gate passing).

- **2026-06-19 — Document Vault: fix "This content is blocked" preview (same-origin reads) (this PR).** Reza prod screenshots: viewing a My Vault document showed Chrome's *"This content is blocked"* and the card image wouldn't render. Root cause: with a GCS service-account key present, `getDocumentReadUrl` handed the browser a native cross-origin `storage.googleapis.com` v4 signed URL; the preview frames it in an `<iframe>` (Chrome blocks cross-origin → `ERR_BLOCKED_BY_RESPONSE`) and GCS serves it with its own content-type (an `<img>` of an `octet-stream` object renders broken). Fix: route EVERY server-backed read (MONITRAX + GCS, key OR keyless) through our same-origin `/api/documents/download` route, which sets `Content-Type` from the DB `mimeType` + `Content-Disposition: inline` and streams GCS bytes server-side. Dropped the native-GCS-signed-URL branch + unused `gcsHasSigningKey` (§12.1). Better CDR posture (every read mediated). `readUrl.test.ts` updated to pin the new contract; `tests/documents` 59/59; tsc + build green. Pure read-path — no schema/migration/calc touch.

- **2026-06-19 — Wealth Universe WX.6.1: tap to zoom first, details second (this PR).** Reza prod screenshots (×3) on his Renew structure: clicking YOU on Level 1 zoomed into its class bundles AND opened the entity detail file at the same time — the file greyed out the universe just zoomed into, forcing a close before the bundles were visible. *"First click on a layer change needs to only zoom in; second click should open the node details."* Fix in both canvases' tap handlers: a FIRST tap on an expandable bubble zooms in ONLY (`selectedId` cleared → no panel); the detail file opens on the SECOND tap of the centred bubble (entity/group); a cluster bubble has no file so its second tap zooms back out instead; a leaf asset or holding-less entity (nothing to zoom into) opens its file on the first tap (unchanged); deep-link `?focus=` mirrors a first tap. §18.2.1 true tweak (interaction-only, no new composition) — code-first, no Stitch. Pure-presentation — no schema/migration/calc touch. Layout tests 36/36 unchanged. Follow-up to WX.6 (PR #1158).

- **2026-06-18 — Wealth Universe WX.6: entity → class → asset zoom (Level-2 asset-class bundles) (this PR).** Reza prod report on his 8-entity Renew structure: focusing an entity that holds many assets dumped ~25 raw tiles at once — *"the first layer is messy … each entity or asset class should be bundled up into 1 bubble and after I click … it can zoom in to the next layer."* Reza chose the **Entity → class → asset** 3-level model. The dormant cluster-by-type mechanism (previously gated to ≤2-entity universes) is now also applied **inside a focused entity** for multi-entity universes: tapping an entity unfolds **per-class bundle bubbles** (Properties / Loans / Vehicles / Shares / Cash) carrying count + class total; a class with a single asset renders that asset directly (a "1 Property" bundle is noise — psychology lens); loans read "$X owing" and stay out of the held total (debt is not held wealth — financial-adviser lens). Tapping a bundle opens the existing L3 `cluster-<entityId>-<kind>` focus scene. **`expandedEntityIds` is now a navigation STACK** (last id = active focus) so each tap descends one layer and the breadcrumb renders every layer as a tappable crumb (`Universe ‹ Reza ‹ Properties`), with the centred bubble popping ONE layer. Both canvases (`WealthUniverseCanvas` + `WealthUniverseMobile`). Stitch-first per §18.2.1: desktop-dark `24620f695c144a0b91ce43ac70a2497b` + mobile-dark `4bcd4f111f504ffc8d601ddb2219eca4` (project `1859462351962811110`), artefacts at `.stitch/designs/wealth-universe-l2-bundles/`. 6 new tests (42 in the suite; updated the old "two-ring at entity focus" test → now L3). Pure-presentation + pure-layout — no schema, no migration, no calc-engine touch. Design-spike PR #1157 (artefact only) merged first; this is the implementation per Reza's "build it in a new PR".

- **2026-06-18 — DME D.3 (policy SSOT): canonical `confidencePolicy.ts` + threshold unification (this PR).** New `lib/documents/intelligence/confidencePolicy.ts` is the ONE source for the document-intelligence confidence thresholds (AUTO ≥0.9 / CONFIRM ≥0.7 / ASK <0.7) + autonomy band + label/tone, with an earned-autonomy TRAIL-stage downgrade (Track/Reduce never AUTO). Replaces the 0.9/0.7 magic numbers duplicated across `GlobalScanReceipt`/`AnalysisPreviewCard`/`ExtractionReviewForm` (§12.3). 6 tests; tsc + build green. **Deliberately deferred — auto-EXECUTION** (auto-writing a financial record when band===AUTO) is NOT wired: it's a §12.11 + financial-adviser call + Stitch UX (undo), pending Reza sign-off. **Next: D.4 learned routing → D.5 lifecycle.**

- **2026-06-18 — DME D.2: record reconciliation in the document-confirm flow (this PR).** The `/api/documents/analyze/confirm` create functions wrote new Expense/Income/Loan rows with NO dedup — confirming the same document twice, or two docs for one bill, duplicated records. New canonical `lib/documents/intelligence/reconcile/reconcileSuggestedAction.ts`: before a CREATE_* writes, it checks for an existing matching record (expense: user+amount+vendor/name scoped to linked property/loan/asset; income: +type; loan: name+principal). On a match the confirm route links the document to the EXISTING record (`reconciled:true`) instead of duplicating, and skips the receipt-matcher so no duplicate transaction is synthesised. SSOT — one place decides 'already in the system?'; generalises D.1's `/api/expenses` `dedupeReceipt` sibling. 7 unit tests; tsc + build green; no schema change. **Next: D.3 confidence-gated autonomy → D.4 learned routing → D.5 lifecycle.**

- **2026-06-17 — Documents: persistent "Add as expense" + tag de-clutter (this PR).** Reza testing found (1) the recognised-banner "Add as expense" was in-memory only — leaving the page lost the chance to turn the receipt into an expense; (2) the doc row showed "Receipt" (category) AND "receipt" (tag) plus technical tags ("api-direct", "asset"). **Fix 1:** a per-document **"$ Add as expense"** action on RECEIPT/INVOICE docs reads the SAVED analysis (`GET /api/documents/analyze?documentId`) and creates the expense (dedup'd + doc-linked) — works any time, survives navigation; refactored into one shared `createExpenseFrom(rec)` used by both the banner and the per-doc action (§12.1). **Fix 2:** `DocumentList.visibleTags()` hides auto-tags that duplicate the category badge / entity badges / upload source (`SOURCE_TAGS` denylist); user tags still show. tsc + build green; no schema change. **Then (Reza answers, same PR):** issue #2 → **real thumbnails** (`RowThumb` renders the actual image for image docs, icon fallback); issue #3 edit → **full edit dialog** (name + category) via new `updateDocumentMeta` + `action:'update'` PATCH. **All three testing issues resolved.**

- **2026-06-17 — DME D.1: document-level dedup at the single chokepoint + single-engine clarification (this PR).** Reza challenged the "DME 2.0 / two engines" wording (strict single-engine principle, §12.3). Clarified + corrected the Phase 50 doc: there is ONE Document **Management** Engine (filing) and ONE Document **Intelligence** Engine (reading) — separate concerns, one implementation each (the same "two SSOTs, not duplication" distinction §12.2 draws for snapshots); Phase D extends them, it's NOT a new engine. Then built **D.1**: `DocumentManagementEngine.processUpload` SHA-256-hashes the bytes and, before storing, looks up `(userId, contentHash)` — on a byte-identical match it returns the existing document flagged `duplicate` (adding any new links) instead of storing a second copy; the upload route skips re-analysis and `DocumentsSection` shows "you already uploaded this — linked it here". Stops the "same receipt twice" at the SOURCE (not just the downstream expense). Schema: `Document.contentHash` + `(userId, contentHash)` index + additive migration `20260617150000` (§12.12 ✓, non-destructive §12.11). tsc + build green. **Deferred (D.1.1):** funnel the legacy scan path (`documentService.uploadDocument`) through the DME so it shares the one dedup; near-duplicate fingerprinting. **Next (Reza-approved order):** D.2 record reconciliation → D.3 confidence-gated autonomy → D.4 learned routing → D.5 lifecycle.

- **2026-06-17 — AI-on-upload propagated to Properties + Investments; dedup generalised; DME 2.0 design (this PR).** Reza directive: add real "AI smarts" to the Document Management Engine to avoid issues like duplicate receipts, document it, and apply the recent doc features to all wealth items. (1) **Propagation:** `analyzeOnUpload` now on the Properties (`properties/[id]`) and Investment-account (`investments/accounts/[id]`) Documents sections (was Assets only) — uploads OCR + auto-rename; Properties also get "Add as expense" with dedup; Investments OCR+rename only. (2) **Dedup generalised:** `/api/expenses` flag is now `dedupeReceipt` (`dedupeOnAsset` kept as alias) and matches on whichever entity link is present (asset/property/loan/investment), not just asset. (3) **Design:** new **Phase D — AI-powered DME 2.0** section in `PHASE_50_AI_DOCUMENT_ROUTER.md` documenting what the DME/DIE already do (routing/OCR/classification/extraction/suggested-actions, but NO dedup/judgement), and a phased plan for the missing "smarts" layer — document-level hash dedup (D.1, recommended next), record reconciliation (D.2), confidence-gated autonomy (D.3), learned routing (D.4), lifecycle intelligence (D.5). tsc + build green; no schema change. **Awaiting Reza go-ahead on the D.1–D.5 order.** **Deferred:** per-expense list parity on Properties/Investments detail pages (their expenses live in `/dashboard/expenses`).

- **2026-06-17 — Receipt→expense dedup + doc↔expense link + per-expense delete (SSOT fix) (this PR).** Reza found a non-SSOT bug: "Add as expense" created a standalone expense with no doc link and no dedup, so uploading several photos of the same receipt made duplicate expense rows, and deleting the duplicate files left the expenses orphaned. Fixes: (1) **server-side dedup** — `/api/expenses` POST gains an opt-in `dedupeOnAsset` flag that returns an existing expense (same asset+amount+vendor/name, `duplicate:true`) instead of creating a duplicate; manual entry unaffected (SSOT — guard in the canonical API). (2) **doc↔expense link** — `addExpenseFromRecognised` links the document to the created/existing expense via `/api/documents/[id]/link`. (3) **per-expense delete** on the asset Expenses tab (was edit+attach only) so duplicates can be cleaned up — manual + confirmed (Reza decision: NO auto-cascade on document delete; financial records shouldn't vanish when a photo is removed, §12.11). (4) receipts default to **ANNUAL** not MONTHLY (MONTHLY 12×'d a one-off in cashflow). (5) banner shows "already exists — linked this receipt to it" on dedup. No NEW destructive Prisma write (dedup is a read; delete reuses the existing endpoint). tsc + build green. **Deferred:** true ONE_OFF expense frequency (schema), and moving dedup+autorename+AI server-side so the global scan flow benefits.

- **2026-06-17 — Auto-rename documents from AI recognition (this PR).** Reza feedback after recognition started working: the uploaded file kept its camera name ("IMG_6615.JPG"). On a successful `analyzeOnUpload` recognition, `DocumentsSection` now auto-renames the document to a meaningful AI-derived name — `"{vendor} - {date}.{ext}"` (e.g. "QBE Insurance - 2026-06-18.jpg"), falling back to the document type when no vendor is found. Reuses the existing `PATCH /api/documents/[id]` `rename` action; best-effort (a rename failure never breaks the upload). Organises the Vault by what the document IS rather than the camera filename. tsc + build green; no schema change. **Deferred:** apply the same auto-rename server-side in the upload route so the global "Scan a receipt" flow benefits too.

- **2026-06-17 — Fix: Documents AI-recognise crash (React #31) + truncated filename (this PR).** Reza testing: clicking "Upload document" on the asset Documents tab crashed the whole page ("Something went wrong"), and the filename rendered as "I…". **Crash root cause:** the upload route's `analysis.extractedData` stores fields as the engine's `ExtractedField` shape (`{value, confidence}`); `DocumentsSection.pickField` returned the raw object and the recognised banner rendered it → React error #31 ("Objects are not valid as a React child"). Fix: `pickField` unwraps `.value` and refuses to return any object/array (only primitives are renderable). **Filename fix:** in `DocumentList` the filename shared one row with 4 action buttons (squeezed to one char on mobile) → moved the filename to its own full-width line with metadata + compact `h-8 w-8` icon buttons below. tsc + build green; no schema change.

- **2026-06-17 — Fix: asset detail/edit dialogs overflow mobile width (this PR).** After #1138 the polished KPI tiles rendered (proving a fresh bundle — the app has no service worker, so it was never a cache issue) but the dialog still ran off the right edge of the phone. Root cause: the shadcn `DialogContent` is `display:grid`, and its grid-item children (KPI grid, Tabs) carry `min-width:auto`, letting them push the dialog wider than the viewport. Fix: explicit `w-[calc(100vw-1rem)]` + `overflow-x-hidden` + **`[&>*]:min-w-0`** on the detail AND edit dialogs (every grid child may shrink → none can force the dialog wide); plus the edit form's vehicle-details row `grid-cols-3` → `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3`. tsc + build green; no schema change.

- **2026-06-17 — Asset detail mobile redesign (Stitch-first, approved) (this PR).** Reza testing showed the asset detail (a desktop `max-w-4xl` dialog) clipping on mobile — KPI tiles + tabs overflowed — and directed it through Stitch per §18.2.1 (correcting the earlier #1137 code-first "true tweak"). Generated a mobile design (project 1859462351962811110, screen `770abf40f2584174b52db63a4310a34a`), Reza **approved**, then converted to React: `DialogContent` now `overflow-x-hidden p-4 sm:p-6`; gradient car-badge header with truncating title; new polished **`KpiTile`** glass component (§18.7.2 recipe — hairline + 3px gradient top-accent + tabular-nums + `min-w-0`/`truncate` so long numbers never overflow), **2×2 on mobile / 4-across on sm+**; TabsList horizontally scrollable with hidden scrollbar. Artefact `.stitch/designs/asset-detail-mobile/`. tsc + build green; no schema change. **Companion diagnosis (no code):** Reza's "AI still not picking uploads" — prod logs showed the asset upload sent `analyze: false` on the #1137 deploy → STALE cached client bundle (pending PWA "Relaunch to update") or the expense-row paperclip (files-only); the server fix (analyzeOnUpload + REST Vision) is correct + live. Resolution: relaunch + use Documents tab → Upload. **Deferred:** propagate the polished mobile detail + analyzeOnUpload to Properties/Investments; consider migrating the asset detail from a dialog to the §18.7.5 Asset Spotlight page.

- **2026-06-17 — Asset Documents: AI recognition on upload + mobile framing fix (this PR).** Reza testing on the Toyota Landcruiser detail: (1) the asset Documents "Upload document"/"Take photo" didn't engage AI vision (the per-item `DocumentsSection` uploaded with no `analyze`), and (2) the detail dialog was poorly framed on mobile (KPI tiles `grid-cols-4` clipped "Depreciation"; 5-tab `TabsList` overflowed). **Fix 1:** added an opt-in `analyzeOnUpload` prop to `DocumentsSection` — when set, the single entity-linked upload also passes `analyze=true`, runs Vision OCR, and surfaces the recognised vendor/amount/date inline with a one-tap "Add as expense for <asset>" (creates an expense linked to the asset/property; the doc is already filed under the item). Enabled on the asset detail Documents tab. **Fix 2:** KPI grid → `grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4` (2×2 on mobile), TabsList → `flex w-full justify-start overflow-x-auto` (scrollable). tsc + build green; no schema change. - **2026-06-17 — Scan-a-receipt → attach to an asset/property + Vault Assets folder (this PR).** Reza testing (post-OCR-fix): the "Scan a receipt" result screen only had "Add expense", which created a standalone `GENERAL` expense with no entity link — so a QBE-insurance receipt for the Toyota Landcruiser asset couldn't be attached to it, and the document filed under generic Expenses instead of the asset. **Added a "What is this for?" selector** (assets + properties, Reza scope decision) to the result screen: on confirm it (a) creates the expense with the chosen `assetId`/`propertyId` (the `/api/expenses` POST already accepts these) and (b) links the saved document to that entity via `POST /api/documents/[id]/link`, so both the expense AND the receipt file under the item. **Also fixed issue #3** — added the missing **Assets** branch to the Vault FolderTree (`UserEntities.assets` + `getAllUserEntities`/`lookupEntities` now fetch assets + the `entity:ASSET:*` counts/filtering were already generic). Stitch §18.2.1 backfill (code-first, Reza-approved): project 1859462351962811110 screen `20c36c03c45c41ceaa6e3d070c40ba60`, artefact `.stitch/designs/scan-link-asset/`. tsc + build green; no schema change (LinkedEntityType.ASSET already existed). **Deferred:** loans/investments as scan targets; making the per-expense camera/attach button also OCR.

- **2026-06-17 — Vision OCR: force REST transport (`fallback: true`) — THE root cause of "AI not working" (this PR).** Live prod-log capture of Reza's receipt scan was the unlock: Vision authenticated fine with the restored key (`[Vision] Using service account: monitrax-backend@monitrax-479700…`) but the OCR call threw `Error: undefined undefined: undefined` (`code: undefined`, gax `note: 'Exception occurred in retry method that was not classified as transient'`, stack in gRPC `onReceiveStatus`/`callErrorFromStatus`). That is the **gRPC/HTTP-2 transport failing in Vercel's bundled serverless runtime** — the request dies before reaching Google. **This explains the entire saga's red herring:** "Vision API enabled but ZERO traffic" was never an auth problem (key vs keyless was irrelevant); every call failed at the transport layer. **Fix:** construct `ImageAnnotatorClient` with `fallback: true` (REST/HTTP instead of gRPC) on all three auth branches — the standard fix for Google client libs on serverless. tsc + build green; no schema change. **Telemetry follow-up (#2):** the GCP sinks (Cloud Logging/Monitoring/Error Reporting/SCC/Scheduler) are also gRPC clients and likely fail the same way on Vercel — restoring the key stopped the crash/no-op, but making them FUNCTION needs the same REST transport. **Diagnostics lesson:** the Vercel MCP log tool truncates to the first line per request; the full gRPC error only came from streaming `runtime-logs` (NDJSON) with repeated short connects to flush the buffer — record this technique for future prod debugging.

- **2026-06-17 — Vision auth back to KEY-FIRST + restore the GCS key (this PR).** Live prod diagnosis of Reza's mobile receipt upload proved the keyless cutover was premature: keyless GCS fails in prod with `Anonymous caller does not have storage.buckets.get access` (401) — the keyless `authClient` is not honoured by `@google-cloud/storage` at runtime (cross-package google-auth-library instance). Storage + telemetry sinks are key-first, so restoring `GCS_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY` fixes them with zero code change; but `visionService.ts` was made keyless-first in #1131, so it would keep hitting the broken keyless path even after the key returns. **Fix:** flip Vision back to **key-first** (key → keyless WIF → ADC), matching the storage provider + `lib/gcp/credentials.ts`. One env var (`GCS_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY`) now governs every GCP client consistently; keyless stays as the no-key fallback, to be re-enabled only once verified end-to-end. **Sequencing lesson (the deletion was premature):** retire a working credential only AFTER its replacement is proven in prod with the old one still in place as fallback — the storage code's key-first precedence meant keyless only engaged once the key was gone, i.e. we removed the safety net to test the unproven path in prod. **Operator action:** restore the key (Production scope); merging this PR deploys the key + the Vision fix together. tsc + build green; no schema change. **Also clarified:** the per-expense camera/attach uploads with `analyze:false` (files only, no OCR by design) — AI recognition runs via the global "Scan a receipt" flow.

- **2026-06-17 — CI: serialize vitest to kill the Prisma-engine native abort (root-cause fix) (this PR).** The vitest job kept aborting with a native Prisma query-engine crash (exit 134, "Failed to deserialize constructor options") — a CI-infra flake, never a real test failure. First attempt (2026-06-16) added a whole-suite retry (`npm run test || npm run test`), since the *process* crash can't be caught by vitest's `test.retry`. That proved **insufficient**: on 2026-06-17 the flake hit **both** parallel runs (2× "Aborted (core dumped)", 2× retry warnings). **Root cause:** the panic is triggered by **concurrent PrismaClient / N-API engine construction across vitest's default parallel fork pool**. **Primary fix:** `npm test` now runs `vitest run --no-file-parallelism` (test files execute sequentially → no concurrent engine construction → the trigger is gone). Verified locally: full suite **152 files / 2897 tests green in 45s** (vs ~30s parallel — +15s is the cost of determinism). The whole-suite retry is kept as cheap residual insurance. Real failures stay deterministic and still fail. Workflow + `package.json` `test` script only; no app code.

- **2026-06-16 — Fix: GCP sinks crash with uncaughtException under keyless (prod) (this PR).** After removing `GCS_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY` for the keyless cutover, prod logs showed `Uncaught Exception` across many routes (`/api/documents/[id]`, `/api/assets/[id]`, `portfolio/snapshot`, …). Root cause: `lib/gcp/credentials.ts` `getGCPCredentials()` fell through to a **credential-less ADC options object**; the secondary GCP sinks (Cloud Logging / Monitoring / Error Reporting / Scheduler + audit Cloud-Logging mirror) then constructed a client that **fails ADC asynchronously** (no metadata server on Vercel), throwing an uncaughtException that escaped their fire-and-forget `.catch()` and destabilised in-flight requests — the SAME failure #1121 gated for the E2E emulator only; this extends the guard to **prod keyless**. Fix: `getGCPCredentials()` returns `null` when there's no key + no `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` + the WIF env is present → those sinks no-op (they all handle null). The audit log's Postgres **primary** is unaffected. Likely the destabiliser behind the broken document preview. Follow-up: make the GCP sinks authenticate via keyless WIF. tsc + build green; no schema change.

- **2026-06-16 — Keyless Vision OCR (fixes the broken AI scan, issue #5) (PR #1131, merged).** Root cause of the scan/Vision failure: `visionService.ts` authenticated with the legacy `monitrax-backend` service-account key — the **same key that proved unable to reach the Vision API** (API enabled, but zero traffic → auth failing before the call). Reworked Vision to **prefer keyless Workload Identity Federation** (the `vercel-monitrax-db` identity the DB + keyless GCS use) → key → ADC, mirroring the GCS provider. `isAvailable()` now accepts keyless WIF so removing the key for the cutover doesn't disable OCR. tsc/build/gate clean; no schema change. **⛔ Operator action to make it work:** grant `vercel-monitrax-db` permission to call the Vision API (`roles/serviceusage.serviceUsageConsumer` on `monitrax-479700`); then it OCRs keyless. This also unblocks removing `GCS_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY` (the storage cutover behind the broken-preview issue #2). **Still open:** #1 docs-under-expense (UI/API), #3 vault asset-folder (FolderTree has no ASSET + path-rule priority).

Added: (1) `HALF_YEARLY` → "Half-yearly" in the asset-expense frequency dropdown; (2) **edit an existing asset expense** — a pencil button on each expense row opens the dialog pre-filled (`openEditExpense`), `handleExpenseSubmit` now PUTs `/api/expenses/[id]` when editing (POST when adding), dialog title + CTA reflect Add vs Edit, Cancel/reset clears the edit state. `AssetExpense` gains optional `isTaxDeductible` so edits don't silently drop it. tsc/build/gate clean; storage-independent (no link to the still-open preview/folder/Vision cluster). **Still open from the same testing round:** docs not shown under the expense row (C), preview broken (A) + vault asset-folder (B) + AI Vision (E) — investigated together as the GCS/storage-auth-rooted cluster.

Documents tab unchanged. Plain store+link (no AI dependency). **(3) Camera everywhere:** new `CameraCaptureDialog` (getUserMedia — desktop webcam AND mobile); `DocumentsSection` now always shows Take-photo (native `capture` on touch, webcam dialog on desktop) — was hidden on non-touch. 3 rename tests, 22/22 documents tests; tsc/build/gate clean; §12.12 N/A. Next: **Super** per-item upload; **mobile-scan (keyless Vision)** fix.

- **2026-06-16 — Per-item Documents: upload/scan ON the wealth items (this PR).** Reza (frustrated): upload existed only on My Vault + a few dialogs — **Properties, Investments, Super, Assets had NO upload** (per-item upload had been deferred to a never-built "Phase C"). Shipped a reusable `components/documents/DocumentsSection.tsx` (glass card: upload + mobile "Take photo" `capture="environment"` + list/view/delete), mounted on **Properties detail**, **Investment-account detail**, and the **Assets dialog** (new Documents tab). Uploads link to the item (`assetId`/`propertyId`/`investmentAccountId`) AND — via a **new `asset_path` storage rule** — file under `${userId}/assets/${assetId}/...` (asset docs previously fell through to a generic folder). **Deliberately bypasses the AI form-autofill path** so it works even while Vision OCR is down. 3 new tests (asset-folder rule), 19/19 documents tests green; tsc/build/gate clean. §12.11/§12.12 N/A. **Super deferred** (needs a `SUPER` LinkedEntityType + migration) → fast-follow. Mobile-scan recognition (keyless Vision) is the separately-sequenced next fix.

- **2026-06-16 — Fix: storage factory GCS-detection must accept keyless (this PR).** Caught live during GCS provisioning (Reza: *"are you sure deleting the key won't break the app?"* — it would have). The keyless PR (#1126) made the *provider* keyless-capable but left the *factory's* `isGCSConfigured` gate requiring `GCS_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY` — so deleting the key would have silently routed new uploads to the **DB** (no crash, wrong backend). New pure `computeGcsConfigured(env)`: GCS configured when project+bucket set AND (key **OR** keyless WIF env `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER`+`GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL`). Backward-compatible (key still set → identical behaviour); 5 tests. **Documented in detail for rollback** (Reza directive): PHASE_50 § "Storage backend — how it resolves, and how to roll back" (resolution order, IAM per mode, zero-downtime cutover, failure-mode table, emergency rollback = re-add the key). **Cutover state:** bucket + both SAs (key `monitrax-backend`, keyless `vercel-monitrax-db`/`objectUser`) ready; after merge → delete `GCS_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY` + redeploy → keyless engages.

- **2026-06-16 — Phase 4 Layer 4: `playwright (UAT)` job GREEN under the Firebase Auth emulator (this PR / #1121).** Reproduced the CI job locally (Postgres + Auth emulator + `seed:lighthouse` archetypes + a real prod build) and drove the 4 UAT flows to green (2 consecutive 9s runs). **Root causes fixed:** (1) **`lib/gcp/credentials.ts` ADC uncaught exception** — with `GCP_PROJECT_ID` set (the E2E job sets it) but no key, `getGCPCredentials()` returned a credential-less options object, so the Cloud Logging audit sink constructed and then failed ADC *asynchronously*, surfacing an `uncaughtException` that escaped the sink's fire-and-forget `.catch()` and aborted `/api/master-snapshot` → dashboard hung on "Loading…". Gated to return null under `FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST` (set only in the job; prod/preview byte-for-byte unchanged; vitest 2870✓ unaffected). The CSP `connect-src` fix from earlier on the branch was confirmed effective — login reaches `/dashboard`. (2) **Blocking modals intercepted every click** — the seeded archetypes had `onboardingCompleted=false` (OnboardingWelcomeModal) **and** no current-version legal consent (`ConsentMigrationModal`); each renders a full-screen Radix overlay that persists across client nav and swallows pointer events. `seed-emulator.ts` now marks onboarding complete + writes the 3 mandatory `UserConsent` rows at the current doc version (E2E-only). (3) **Spec robustness** (`uat.spec.ts`): wait for the auth shell + generous `LOAD` ceiling (the `master-snapshot`/scenario compute takes several seconds); scope the add-property dialog past the persistent feedback/help `role="dialog"` asides; fill all `required` form fields; the sellProperty lever auto-selects the *largest* property (the family PPR — CGT-exempt) so the spec picks an **investment** property and asserts the per-owner CGT breakdown in the "How we computed this" panel (the literal `Estimated CGT (your share)` is an internal impact label the lever summarises, not visible text); `playwright.config` gains `reducedMotion: 'reduce'` (framer-motion elements never reach Playwright's "stable" state otherwise) + a 120s per-test timeout. Job stays **non-required** until green in CI; once CI confirms, add **`playwright (UAT)`** as a required status check on the `main` ruleset. Emulator/test wiring remains strictly env-gated (never prod/preview); synthetic data only.

- **2026-06-16 — AI Document Router · Phase B (storage track): keyless GCS auth + GCS-aware download (PR #1126, merged & prod-verified).** Reza chose the keyless route (recommended) — no static service-account key; GCS reuses the same Workload Identity Federation identity the DB uses (§13.6). New canonical `lib/gcp/wifAuthClient.ts` (`buildGcpWifAuthClient()` → `external_account` IdentityPoolClient minting impersonated SA tokens from the Vercel OIDC token; byte-identical to `lib/db.ts`'s inline client, kept separate to avoid touching CDR-critical DB auth). GCS provider `initialize` now resolves auth: key → keyless WIF → ADC. `/api/documents/download` rewritten provider-aware (verify HMAC → look up Document by storagePath → stream from DB bytea or GCS — the old route could only serve DB files). New read-URL policy SSOT `lib/documents/storage/readUrl.ts`: GCS+key → native v4 signed URL; **GCS keyless → our HMAC streaming route** (keyless can't sign locally; bytes never leave via a public URL = better CDR posture); wired into `documentService`. 4 tests (11 with quota). §12.11/§12.12 N/A. **Operator action to go live (now just 3 steps, no secret): create bucket `monitrax-documents` (australia-southeast1) → grant the runtime SA `roles/storage.objectAdmin` on it → set `GCS_PROJECT_ID` + `GCS_BUCKET_NAME` in Vercel.** The analyze-500 stays a separate diagnostic.

- **2026-06-16 — Doc-sync sweep for Phase 50 (AI Document Router) (PR #1125).** Reza directive: *"make sure all relevant documents are updated as you go, including the implementation plan and support documents."* Caught the support-doc gap the continuity-gate flags (workstream changes must pair with STATE.md): **STATE.md** cursor re-pinned to HEAD `654bc55` (Doc Router now the active line; GCS provisioning = the next operator action; decisions logged); **`00_INDEX.md`** + **`MASTER_BLUEPRINT.md`** phase table now list **Phase 50**; **`09_INFRASTRUCTURE_AND_DEPLOYMENT.md`** documents the storage backend (factory auto-selects GCS once the 3 env vars are set; DB-bytea fallback otherwise) + the pending prod provisioning. Docs-only, no code.

- **2026-06-16 — AI Document Router · Phase B (storage track, slice 1): per-user storage quota (PR #1124, merged & prod-verified).** Reza decision: document storage = **GCS, with a per-user quota** — this ships the quota half (the GCS switch itself is operator-provisioning + the storage factory's existing auto-select, no code). New canonical `lib/documents/storage/storageQuota.ts` — **drift-free** (usage COMPUTED from `SUM(Document.size) WHERE deletedAt IS NULL`, never a maintained counter), **backend-independent** (counts DB bytea + GCS the same), default **2 GiB** with a per-user override hook for later. Enforced at the single DME `processUpload` chokepoint **and** the legacy `documentService.uploadDocument` (scan) path so no upload bypasses it; LOCAL_DRIVE exempt (bytes on the user's machine). `/api/documents/upload` maps a breach to **413** via a new `EngineResult.errorCode` (not a generic 500). 7 unit tests. **§12.11/§12.12 N/A** (no destructive write, no schema change — quota reads the existing `size` column). Spec: new `docs/blueprint/PHASE_50_AI_DOCUMENT_ROUTER.md` (full A/B/C plan + GCS provisioning checklist + decisions log). **Next:** GCS-aware `/api/documents/download` (DB-only today), then B-intelligence (`ATTACH_TO_*` actions + entity picker + Stitch scan-UI "attach vs create" branch). **⛔ GCS cut-over blocked on operator provisioning** (bucket + SA grant + 3 Vercel env vars — PHASE_50 §B-storage).

- **2026-06-16 — AI Document Router · Phase A: fix scan recognition + `ASSET` linkable type (PR #1123, merged & prod-verified).** Reza's vision (approved): every doc/receipt → AI recognises → attaches to the correct item/asset OR creates a new item/expense → filed in the right Vault folder for reporting/extraction. 3-phase build; this is **Phase A (foundation + the live bug)**. **(1) Scan recognition fix:** Reza scanned a receipt and it only stored to Vault without identifying it. Prod logs showed `POST /api/documents/analyze` **500s** on a fresh photo (reaches OCR then throws; storage-read + Vision-config both eliminated via log queries). The scan's two-step path (upload → re-read stored file → analyze) is unreliable in prod, so **`GlobalScanReceipt` was rewired to the proven single-step `/api/documents/analyze-for-form`** — the exact path the expense/income forms use successfully (OCRs the in-hand upload + Gemini-maps + files as RECEIPT). Result preview now renders vendor/amount/GST/date/category from `fieldMappings`; "Add expense" creates via `/api/expenses`. **(2) `ASSET` linkable document type:** previously docs couldn't link to assets (no `ASSET` in `LinkedEntityType`), so a receipt couldn't tag to a vehicle. Added end-to-end: enum (schema + migration `20260616093000_add_asset_linked_entity_type` + TS mirror), `EntityContext.assetId`, upload-route entity map, `useDocumentUpload` `LINK_FIELD_BY_ENTITY`, new `asset_direct` DME LinkingRule, DocumentList label map — the foundation Phase C's asset Documents section builds on. **§12.11** N/A (additive `ADD VALUE`); **§12.12** migration paired. Verified: tsc clean, build passes, lint clean (1 pre-existing `<img>` warning). **Completeness review delivered** (HEIC, dedupe, owning-entity link, Tax-pack + ATO retention, renewal tie-in, per-item Documents sections, security/PII, storage-at-scale, lifecycle) → sequenced into Phase B/C. Stitch design for the asset Documents section generated (`.stitch/designs/asset-documents/`, dark) pending Phase C build. Strategic decisions logged: **storage = GCS** (Phase B track); **household = shared finances incl. documents** if/when multi-user accounts exist.

- **2026-06-16 — `HALF_YEARLY` frequency, added everywhere a frequency is picked (PR #1122, merged).** The frequency dropdown (insurance especially) only offered Weekly/Fortnightly/Monthly/Quarterly/Annually — no half-yearly, despite many AU bills billing every 6 months. Added `HALF_YEARLY` ("Half-yearly", 2×/yr) **end-to-end** to the two billing-cadence enums **`Frequency`** (expenses/income UI) + **`PayFrequency`** (income/tax). **Correctness was the hard part:** every annualisation switch that consumes these enums had to learn the value or it would silently mis-annualise (a $215.59 half-yearly premium must be $431.18/yr, not $215.59). Updated the canonical converter (`lib/utils/frequencies.ts` — toAnnual/periodsPerYear/toAnnualDecimal) **plus 9 independent switches** that re-derive frequency without it: PAYG calculator ×4 (Float+Decimal weekly conversions + binary-search), `reports/contextBuilder` ×2, `intelligence/portfolioEngine` ×2, `testing/exporter` ×2, `utils/reconciliation`, `ai/debt-analysis`. Plus the enum mirror (`prisma-enums.ts`), Zod (`validation/common.ts`), income API `toPayFrequency` mapping (×2 routes), `tax/route` + `tax/salary` validation/multipliers, the AI form-options list, and **5 UI dropdowns** (ExpenseDialog, CreateExpenseFromRecurring, TransactionLinkDialog ×2, income page) now showing "Half-yearly". **Out of scope (by design, stated to Reza):** `RepaymentFrequency` (loans are W/F/M only), `RecurrencePattern` (auto-detection internals), `DiningFrequency` — verified no `HALF_YEARLY` value can leak into those paths. **Schema change (§12.12):** matching additive migration `20260616074500_add_half_yearly_frequency` (`ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS` — non-destructive, §12.11 N/A). **§12.14:** touches `lib/tax-engine/*` but the added cases are pure annualisation math — outcome (b), no post-reform number change, FW-2 wall intact, 0 tax-engine tests regressed. Verified: `tsc` clean; `npm run build` passes; vitest 64 (frequencies, incl. an insurance-premium correctness guard) + 1035 (tax-engine/cashflow/calculations/intelligence) green; lint clean on 22 changed files. Docs: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` + CHANGELOG_2026_06_16.

- **2026-06-16 — Phase 26.7: mobile camera capture + global "Scan a receipt" (this PR).** The user couldn't find document upload under Assets and thought the AI document recognition was gone — it isn't; the Phase 25/26 engine is fully live but lives in **My Vault**, not where mobile users look. Two additions, both riding the **canonical pipeline** (no new analysis/create logic): **(1) Camera-in-forms (code-first tweak):** `FormDocumentUpload` (Expense/Loan/Income/Balances/Transaction dialogs) gained a native **"Take photo"** button — second hidden input `accept="image/*" capture="environment"`, shown only on touch devices (`pointer: coarse`). Existing file/drop-zone upload unchanged → every dialog now offers snap **and** pick. **(2) Global "Scan a receipt" (Stitch-first, §18.2.1):** new `GlobalScanReceipt` — sky→indigo camera **FAB** above the editorial bottom nav (mobile-only, hidden during onboarding, mounted in `DashboardLayout`) opens a glass bottom sheet: Take photo / Choose a file → `useDocumentUpload` (DME upload as RECEIPT) → `POST /api/documents/analyze` (DIE; **persists** the analysis so it also lands in the Smart Inbox) → result preview (vendor/amount/GST/date/category + confidence pill) → `POST /api/documents/analyze/confirm` (CREATE_EXPENSE). Nothing written until the user confirms; Vision/AI-unavailable degrades to "Saved to your Vault" (no data loss, no shame). Bottom-sheet chrome mirrors `MoreSheet` (Esc + scroll-lock + ARIA + motion-safe; no framer-motion). **Reza scoping decision (2026-06-16):** camera-in-forms + global scan only — Assets-page upload + My-Vault discoverability link deliberately NOT built. Stitch project `1859462351962811110`; screens capture light `895151e084d14c9aaa9dc410e0e3aaba` / dark `cbd9e77e175c4359a7d053dbdd3a5bc1`, result light `87ec42b8ba2f40ccbac4eb2c12d81eb6` / dark `f5a5e9a4068440df868464efca8e7332`; artefacts `.stitch/designs/phase49-scan-receipt/`. **No schema change (§12.12 N/A), no destructive write (§12.11 N/A).** Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (only pre-existing tsconfig baseUrl warning); `npm run build` passes; `npx next lint --file` on all 3 changed files clean. Docs: PHASE_26 §26.7, `06_UI_UX_FOUNDATION.md` (mobile quick-capture pattern), CHANGELOG_2026_06_16. **Open question logged:** the canonical confirm route defaults a scanned receipt to `MONTHLY` frequency (a one-off receipt distorts the budget) — pre-existing, out of scope, flagged for a future fix.

- **2026-06-15 — Phase 3 fix PR2: ownership referential integrity (L2-2) + Entity-Value label (L2-1 Option B) (this PR).** **L2-2:** `OwnershipGroup` / `BeneficialOwnershipOverride` reference assets *polymorphically* (`ownedObjectType` + `ownedObjectId`, no DB FK), so deleting a property/account/investment-account/asset/loan orphaned its ownership rows (no cascade, no app-level cleanup — audit finding). New canonical `lib/services/assetOwnershipCleanup.ts` → `cleanupAssetOwnership(tx, {userId, ownedObjectType, ownedObjectId})` composes two new per-table SSOT deleters (`deleteOwnershipGroupsForAsset` in `ownershipService`, `deleteBeneficialOverridesForAsset` in `beneficialOwnershipService`; `OwnershipStake` cascades via its real FK to the group). Wired into all **5** asset-delete handlers (`properties`/`loans`/`accounts`/`investments/accounts`/`assets` `[id]/route.ts`) **inside a `$transaction`** so the asset + its ownership rows are removed atomically (in-transaction cleanup, the audit's preferred fix over an orphan sweep); the delete audit-log metadata now records the cleaned-up counts. §12.11: each `deleteMany` is confined by a composite `where: {userId, ownedObjectType, ownedObjectId}` to the caller's rows for exactly the asset being deleted. 7 new tests (`tests/services/assetOwnershipCleanup.test.ts`) pin the cleanup contract (both tables hit, correct scoped `where`, aggregated counts, sole-owned 0-row no-op, all 5 types). **L2-1 = Option B (Reza decision):** Entity Value stays computed by **legal title** (binary `ownerEntityId`) — `entityValueBreakdown.ts` math unchanged. Added a factual label on both live entity-value surfaces (the dashboard `EntityBreakdownWidget` footer + the "Entity Value Contribution" chart `sub`): "by legal title — ownership-share & beneficial-owner tax effects are in each entity's Tax view", removing the silent disagreement between the value view and the fractional/beneficial tax view without implying a beneficial-ownership conclusion (AFSL boundary). Label-on-existing-surface, so no Stitch obligation (§18.2.1). **Backlog:** appended the verbatim Phase-3 row **#35** (staged in PR #1112's body, withheld then per F-8) to `03_OPEN_QUESTIONS_AND_BACKLOG.md` + a fix-progress note; logged **Q-ENTITY-VALUE-SHARE** (L2-1 Option A — share-weighted *value*) as a PARKED future product decision. Verified: 0 tsc errors project-wide; 7/7 new tests + ownership/entity-graph suites 83/83; eslint clean (touched files); financial-surfaces lint no new violations.

- **2026-06-15 — Phase 3 fix PR1: canonical asset-value / loan-balance helper (this PR).** Closes audit findings **L1-1 / L1-2 / L1-3** (`docs/audits/PHASE3_ENGINE_CORRECTNESS_2026-06-15.md`) — three read-models re-derived money values on divergent bases instead of importing the canonical valuation. New `lib/calculations/assetValuation.ts` (exported via the calculations barrel): `holdingMarketValue` / `sumHoldingsMarketValue` = `units × (currentPrice || averagePrice)` and `loanBalance` / `sumLoanBalances` = `Number(principal || 0)` — **byte-for-byte the basis `netWorthCalculator` (the untouched SSOT) already uses** (Float path; `Loan.principal` is schema-documented as the current balance). Wired in: (L1-1) `wealthGraphService.sumHoldingsValue` now values holdings via the helper with `currentPrice` added to the holdings select — was using the denormalized `currentValue` cache + cost basis, which drifts from the dashboard when stale; (L1-2) `reports/contextBuilder` investment value (was `averagePrice × units` cost basis) + loan liabilities now route through the helper, with `currentPrice` added to its holdings select; (L1-3) `ai/services/financialAdvisor` loan total routes through `sumLoanBalances` (its snapshot layer has no raw units/price, so the holding helper applies upstream; the net-worth line already prefers canonical `snapshot.netWorth`). **`netWorthCalculator` deliberately untouched.** New `tests/calculations/assetValuation.test.ts` (14 tests) pins each helper's output `===` the canonical `calculateTotalAssets` / `calculateTotalLiabilities` for the same fixtures (live price, cost-basis fallback, fractional units, all loan classes, the advisor `{balance}` shape). Full sweep: 0 tsc errors project-wide; calculations suite 98/98 + related consumer suites 57/57 green. Remaining Phase-3 fix PRs (separate): L2-1 per-entity ownership-value semantics (product + AFSL call for Reza), L2-2 ownership-row referential integrity.

- **2026-06-15 — Phase 47 Stage D · D6: What-If lever per-entity CGT (this PR).** The last Phase 47 build item — the `sellProperty` What-If lever was single-taxpayer (flagged "CGT may apply" without computing it). Now it computes CGT through the canonical per-entity path. New pure core `lib/cfo/scenarios/propertyDisposalCgt.ts` composes `calculateCgtDiscountDecimal` (Div 115 per-entity discount — individual/trust 50%, complying SMSF 33⅓%, company 0%) + the AD-2 `attributeAsset` weighting (TR 93/32: joint tenants split equally, tenants-in-common per recorded share; a beneficial override redirects the whole gain to the beneficial owner), so the lever and the per-entity tax page agree on who owns what share. The lever surfaces an "Estimated CGT (your share)" impact + an after-CGT net-worth line + a per-owner taxable-gain breakdown in the assumptions panel (the existing UI renders impacts + assumptions, so no new section composition → no Stitch obligation per §18.2.1). **Honesty boundary** (financial-adviser + compliance lenses): every owner's *taxable gain* after the correct entity discount is shown (certain from type + holding period); only the **user's own share** is estimated to a tax dollar (current marginal rate, labelled an estimate — a large gain may push into a higher bracket); a co-owner's tax is never fabricated. §12.14 FW-2 preserved — a post-cut-over acquisition surfaces `UC-CGT-POST-REFORM` rather than a silent discounted number. The run route (`/api/cfo/scenarios/run`) builds the per-property ownership + cost-base + marginal-rate context lazily (only for the sellProperty lever); absent it (AI-advisor path / no structure) the lever falls back to the prior CGT flag byte-for-byte. **Stage D build items now complete** (only the registered-tax-agent sign-off, a review gate, remains, scheduled at Basiq prep). 16 D6 tests (`tests/cfo/propertyDisposalCgt.test.ts`); full sweep green except a pre-existing date time-bomb in `taxYearConfig.test.ts` (config `nextReviewBy` of 2026-06-14 is now past — untouched by this PR). Also flipped stale tracker hygiene: Stage F `[~]`→`[x]` (all F1–F4 merged #1090–#1092; the intent of the abandoned PR #1107). tsc + changed-files lint + financial-surfaces lint (0 new violations) clean.

- **2026-06-11 — Phase 45.8: Cashflow redesign (Stitch-first, glass vocabulary) (PR #1064).** Full §18.7.2 glass-vocabulary rewrite of `/cashflow`. Stitch-first 4-variant matrix (project `1859462351962811110`; screens `ff0beab3c934409893fe04441120a472` desktop dark / `60c5d43c95a64920b38a5da2b777faea` desktop light / `be8c24402cad45ae88d55de0daa59781` mobile dark / `ffda21749e004a4daf958ec437b7e35b` mobile light; artefacts `.stitch/designs/cashflow-redesign/`). New composition: hero (confident headline + 4-tile inline KPI row with 30-Day Forecast + Cashflow Health amber pill) + 3 Bento Pairs: Money Flow + Next Best Action (distilled AI summary) / Budget + Tax / Money Leaks **ENHANCED** + Saving Opportunities **NEW**. Money Leaks: in-tile behavioural classifier (duplicate / subscription creep / forgotten subscription / category overspend) — pure mapping over existing leak data, no new backend dep. Saving Opportunities: NEW `lib/cashflow/savingOpportunities.ts` pure detector over the master snapshot — solves Reza's stated pain point ("multiple accounts and properties — hard to pinpoint where money is going and if there are saving opportunities"). v1 covers three high-impact levers: HISA upgrade (2.5pp uplift on liquid cash), offset link (3-month safety buffer + non-investment loan only — never offsets investment loans), salary sacrifice (income > $90k + concessional wedge to $30k FY27 cap). - **QIF import silent-zero-import fix — Gemini 429 resilience + honest import UI.** Branch `claude/serene-goodall-6smazx`. Reza: user’s QIF import “completes” but zero transactions appear; “Gemini has broken a couple of times — fix it one time.” **Diagnosis from prod logs (§17.3):** Gemini categorisation calls hit **429 Too Many Requests** (free-tier quota, ~15 req/min, vs one call per 20 transactions per import); `geminiClient` had no 429 retry (throw-immediately branch); one failed batch discarded all batch results; PR #959’s fallback then sent every transaction to the review-queue black hole (🗑️ row 31) at confidence 0 → `importedCount: 0` + green “Import Complete!”. **Shipped:** (1) `withModelFallbackAndRetry()` in `lib/ai/google/geminiClient.ts` — exponential backoff (1s/2s/4s+jitter ×3) on 429/503/network before model fallback, error-level logging; (2) per-batch failure isolation + `degraded`/`failedBatches` in `categoriseInBatches`; (3) `degraded`/`degradedReason` propagated through `categoriseWithLearning` (the unconfigured branch now logs loudly — was silent); (4) import route logs `[import] AI categorisation DEGRADED…` + returns `aiDegraded`; (5) `TransactionImportDialog` shows the previously-hidden `requiresManual` count, amber “Import received — action needed” header when degraded with 0 imported, and a recovery hint (re-import is safe — duplicates skipped). **Operator action (Reza, GCP):** upgrade project `Monitrax` to paid-tier Gemini quota — steps in runbook `02_ADMIN_TROUBLESHOOTING_RUNBOOK.md` § “QIF import completes but no transactions appear”. Key restrictions from 2026-05-19 ruled out (429 ≠ 403). (b) the Phase 05 custom Resend token module (`lib/security/emailVerification.ts`) stored tokens in an **in-memory `Map`** — fundamentally broken on serverless (token issued on one function instance, verified on another) AND never called during registration anyway. **Decision (Reza 2026-06-10):** soft gate + hard-block CDR; (8) **deleted** `lib/security/emailVerification.ts` (416 lines), `/api/auth/resend-verification`, and the `resend` npm dep (0 remaining usages). §12.11: only writes are one-way false→true flips on `emailVerified`/`emailVerifiedAt`, guarded by the Google-signed claim. ### 2026-05-25
- **Phase 47.66 — Friendlies-tracker automation END-TO-END VERIFIED in production (Monitrax → n8n → Airtable).** Multi-PR debug arc closed today; first real friendly signup landed in Airtable `Signed up` column live. **The PR sequence:**
  - **PR #881 (merged earlier)** — initial build of `lib/webhooks/n8n.ts` + wiring into `app/api/auth/consent/route.ts` and `lib/services/feedbackService.ts`. Signed `user.signup` + `feedback.submitted` events, HMAC-SHA256 signature, originally fire-and-forget.
  - **PR #882 (merged today)** — diagnostic debug log added to `emitFriendliesEvent()` after Reza observed n8n executions weren't firing on real signups. Log surfaced env vars (truthy "set"/length only — never the secret value).
  - **PR #883 (merged today)** — root cause identified from the debug log: **Vercel serverless fire-and-forget bug**. The "invoked" line logged but no follow-up "→ 200/error" line ever fired, because Vercel kills the underlying Node process the moment the API response returns, dropping the in-flight fetch promise. Fix: converted `emitFriendliesEvent` from sync fire-and-forget to `async/await` with a 4s hard timeout (AbortController); both call-sites (`consent/route.ts` + `feedbackService.ts`) now `await` it. Trade-off: signup endpoint blocks up to 4s worst-case if n8n is down, typical sub-second (n8n responseMode: `onReceived` = instant 200). Reza-tested with fresh signup post-merge → Airtable `Test 9` row moved to `Signed up` column. ✅
  - **Commit `dfee7e40` (pushed today, queued for next merge)** — debug `console.log` removed now that the automation is confirmed stable. Operational logs (success `→ 200 OK` + warn/error branches) retained for ongoing visibility.
- **n8n workflow `Monitrax Friendlies Tracker` (`caJUA8f61GHsKEUj`)** — 5 nodes: Webhook trigger → Code (verify HMAC + enrich) → Airtable search → Airtable update → Airtable create. **HMAC verification removed** (Code node sandbox blocks both `require('crypto')` AND the Web Crypto `crypto.subtle` global; re-enable via `NODE_FUNCTION_ALLOW_BUILTIN=crypto` env var on the Hetzner box once SSH access acquired — risk acceptably low: URL is obscure, HTTPS-only, worst-case impact is incorrect Airtable updates).
- **Two outstanding manual UI fixes (Reza-side, ~1 min, can't be safely automated — SDK update would wipe the resourceMapper field config that took multiple iterations to get right):**
  1. **Update Friendly Stage node**: remove `Email` row from field mapping. Current state has `"Email": "="` (empty expression) which wipes the email field on every update — caused Test 9 to land in Signed up with no email visible. Future signups affected until fixed.
  2. **Verify HMAC + Enrich (Code node)**: change `'Feedback given'` → `'Feedback Given'` (capital G — Airtable enum is `Feedback Given`). Won't bite until first feedback submission from a friendly.
- **Backfill guidance:** pre-automation friendlies who already signed up need manual Airtable moves (Invited → Signed up) since the webhook only catches new signups from PR #881 onward. Optional Activity row with `(backfill)` suffix gives clean audit trail.
- **CLAUDE.md §17 compliance verified:** post-merge Vercel deploy verification ran successfully on PRs #881, #882, #883 (`./scripts/vercel-logs.sh list` → READY confirmation + runtime log scan for n8n-webhook output). The session was the structural validation that §17.2 prevents the failure mode of merging a PR with a silent regression.
- **Phase B (queued, ~1h):** n8n Gmail trigger watching `admin@monitrax.com.au` for inbound replies from `friendly`-tagged contacts → writes Airtable Activity row (Type: Inbound Reply). Currently captures send-side; this captures receive-side. **Phase C (queued, ~1h):** Founder Daily Digest enhancement — surface friendly engagement state ("3 friendlies stuck at Invited >7 days", "2 new feedback threads from friendlies", "1 friendly progressed Signed up → Active") in the daily 06:45 email. **The personal voice stays manual** — outbound invites + 2-week follow-up + re-engage + testimonial + referral emails remain hand-written from Reza's Gmail (that's the value, not the friction).

- **Tech Debt #18 — Prod schema-drift CDR tables corrective migration SHIPPED (Day 2 session 5).** **Closes Tech Debt #18 + Phase 0 console row #11.** Reza ran the audit (after PR #806 fixed the endpoint's deserialization bug); JSON showed 3 missing tables + 4 missing enums in prod — all CDR-critical Phase L Fix G18/G43 work that was applied via the historical `prisma db push` workflow and never got a migration file (same root cause as the 2026-05-12 `basiq_connections` drift). New migration `20260519100000_fix_pre_migration_cdr_tables_drift/migration.sql`: 4 `CREATE TYPE` blocks wrapped in `DO $$ ... EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL ... END $$` (Postgres idempotency pattern) + 3 `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` + 3 FK `ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT` in DO blocks + 7 `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`. Purely additive — §12.11 N/A by structural argument. On dev the migration is a complete no-op (every object already exists via `db push`); on prod the missing objects get created in the same `prisma migrate deploy` pass the next deploy runs. Existing consumers (`app/api/admin/cdr/{compliance,complaints,consent}/route.ts`) already have `.catch(() => 0)` defensive wrapping so they degraded gracefully today; once this deploys they'll return real counts. **Pre-Basiq engineering blocker resolved.** No further audit needed unless a future PR adds tables via `db push`.

- **fix(admin): schema-drift endpoint deserialization bug (Day 2 session 4).** Reza tried to run the audit (`GET /api/admin/schema-drift`) — got HTTP 500 with `QUERY_FAILED` + Prisma error "Failed to deserialize column of type 'name'". Root cause: 3 raw SQL queries in `app/api/admin/schema-drift/route.ts` select columns of Postgres's internal `name` type (`information_schema.tables.table_name`, `information_schema.columns.{table_name, column_name}`, `pg_type.typname`, `pg_enum.enumlabel`, `pg_namespace.nspname`) — Prisma's `$queryRawUnsafe` driver can't deserialize this type. Fix: cast each column to `::text AS <alias>` in the SELECT clauses. Three queries fixed. Endpoint was added in PR #743's tooling sweep + never exercised against prod until today. Doc-sync: this Recently Completed entry + `CHANGELOG_2026_05_19.md` Session 4. After deploy, Reza re-runs the audit to get the actual drift JSON.

The 2026-04-10 tech debt was filed when several detail dialogs hadn't been built; Phase 37 + Phase 38 closed the gap. **#6** (`DIRECT_URL` env var) — codebase audit `grep -rn 'DIRECT_URL'` returns ZERO references; the only hit is the tech debt row itself. Prisma `datasource` block only reads `DATABASE_URL`. Genuinely unused → safe to delete from Vercel env (Reza-side action added as console row #22). Net: 2 tech debt rows closed; 1 console todo added.

### 2026-05-18
- **Phase 12 PR 3c.2b — First-visit balance-upgrade migration modal SHIPPED (PR K, session 26).** Closes `PHASE_12_WIZARD_REDESIGN_PLAN.md` §6A.1 item #5. The first-visit story complement to PR F's chip + nudge, PR H's upgrade button, PR I's write-site audit, and PR J's heat-map page. **Schema:** additive `UserPreference.dismissedBalanceUpgradeNudge Boolean @default(false)` + migration `20260518100000_phase_12_pr_3c_2b_balance_upgrade_nudge` — `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` with `NOT NULL DEFAULT false`; Postgres fills the default for every existing row in the same statement (§12.11 N/A by structural argument). **API:** new `GET/POST /api/settings/balance-upgrade-nudge` — mirrors the `reform-banner` route from Phase 41E.3 byte-for-byte (CLAUDE.md §12.2 SSOT — same pattern reviewers + future PRs can copy-paste). **Component:** new `components/onboarding/BalanceUpgradeNudgeModal.tsx` (~170 LOC) — Apple-quiet 3-CTA card: (1) Connect via Basiq (emerald, gated on `useBasiqEnabled()`), (2) Upload statement (sky), (3) Keep entering balances manually (slate). Any of the 3 buttons flips the server flag forward via the new endpoint; modal never auto-shows again. ESC + click-outside both treated as "Keep manual" (still flips flag — not a get-out-of-jail-free skip). **Wiring:** parallel fetch on `/dashboard` mount of dismissal-flag + accounts; gates on (a) user has ≥1 MANUAL account AND (b) not yet dismissed. Both conditions OR-fail → modal never opens. **Lens reads:** behaviour-psychologist — "Keep manual" first-class (not a guilt-trip skip link); users with cash / crypto / foreign accounts who legitimately need MANUAL can pick it without feeling lectured. Designer — restraint; 3 buttons side-by-side, no option visually primary-by-default. Architect — no parallel flows (Connect + Upload reuse existing `/dashboard/balances?action=…` handlers from Phase 36 PR 2b/2c); §12.2 SSOT on the dismissal-flag pattern (mirrors `reform-banner`). Privacy — no balance amounts in copy, only the count of manual accounts surfaces. Footer line points users to Settings > Data Health (PR J) for revisits. No CDR posture change. **Data-source hygiene story now 5/5 §6A.1 items shipped** — only confidence indicators on derived metrics (item §6A.1 #3 — the masterFinancialService touch) still queued.

- **Phase 12 PR 3c.2d — Settings > Data Health (balance-age heat-map) SHIPPED (PR J, session 25).** Closes `PHASE_12_WIZARD_REDESIGN_PLAN.md` §6A.1 item #7. New route `app/dashboard/settings/data-health/page.tsx` — the "single source of truth for staleness" surface that the chip (PR F) + nudge (PR F) link to when the user wants the whole-portfolio view. Composition (CLAUDE.md §12.2 SSOT): hero stat + per-bucket sections + each row reuses the canonical `<DataSourceChip>` + (when applicable) `<UpgradeAccountButton>` from PR H — all three surfaces (list view / detail dialog / data-health page) tell the same story. **Hero stat** ("X% of your N accounts are fresh — Y manual balances are due for a refresh") leads with what's working, not what's broken (CLAUDE.md §0 behaviour-psychologist lens — Bandura self-efficacy; framing the same data as "72% fresh" reads better than "28% stale"). **Heat-map buckets**: Fresh (<14 days, emerald) · Aging (14–60 days, amber) · Stale (≥60 days, rose) · Untracked (no `balanceLastUpdatedAt` — pre-PR-I rows; PR I's audit prevents new ones, slate). Empty buckets self-hide. Each account row: name + institution + `<DataSourceChip>` in compact mode + (for MANUAL/USER_VERIFIED) inline `<UpgradeAccountButton>` in compact mode. Footer guidance line in slate ("Basiq syncs automatically. Imported statements refresh per upload. Manual balances stay frozen until you update them."). New "Data Health" card on the Settings landing page (emerald `<Activity>` icon, description "See which balances are fresh, aging, or stale") deep-links into the route. **No new endpoint** — reads `/api/accounts` directly (same fetch path `/dashboard/balances` uses; the response already includes `balanceSource` + `balanceLastUpdatedAt` by virtue of `findMany` returning every column). Empty state when user has zero accounts. Loading/error states gracefully degrade. Pure read surface — no Prisma writes, no schema change, no API contract change. No CDR posture change.

- **Phase 12 PR 3c.2c — Balance-write audit + canonical helper SHIPPED (PR I, session 24).** Closes `PHASE_12_WIZARD_REDESIGN_PLAN.md` §6A.1 item #6 (app-wide `balanceLastUpdatedAt` write-site audit). The chip + nudge from PR F + the upgrade button from PR H are only honest if every code path that writes `Account.currentBalance` also writes the source + timestamp. This PR is the defensive sweep that makes that invariant a structural property, not a discipline question. **Audit method:** grep every `prisma.account.{create, update, upsert, updateMany}` call in active code (27 hits across 16 files); classify each as "touches currentBalance" vs "doesn't"; for the touchers, verify both `balanceSource` + `balanceLastUpdatedAt` are also written. **Found 3 active gaps + 1 placeholder gap:** (a) `POST /api/accounts` manual create — was creating accounts with no source tag + no timestamp → chip would say "Manual" with no age suffix (the staleness branch never fired). (b) `PATCH /api/accounts/[id]` — when user edits balance, did NOT refresh `balanceLastUpdatedAt` → a freshly-edited manual balance appeared as old as its row's creation date; the staleness nudge would fire on accounts the user JUST updated. (c) `POST /api/basiq/sync` route's local `syncAccount()` helper updates `currentBalance` but didn't tag `balanceSource: 'BASIQ'` → Basiq-synced accounts appeared as default MANUAL in the chip (the chip lied "Manual · 4mo ago" on accounts the sync touched yesterday). (d) `POST /api/accounts/[id]/import` placeholder-create (`currentBalance: 0` before import enrichment) — tagged as IMPORT for chip honesty from the moment the row exists. **Fix:** new SSOT helper `lib/utils/accountBalance.ts` exports `balanceWriteFields(source: BalanceSource, now?: Date): { balanceSource, balanceLastUpdatedAt }`. Caller spreads into Prisma `data`: `data: { currentBalance: 12345, ...balanceWriteFields('USER_VERIFIED') }`. All 4 sites converted. The `PATCH` fix is conditional (`balanceChanging = currentBalance !== undefined`) so a rename / re-type / rate change does NOT falsely refresh the timestamp — that would have flipped a stale-MANUAL chip green for the wrong reason. **7 already-correct sites** left unchanged (`bank/import` × 2, `accounts/[id]/balance`, `bank/basiqSync.ts` worker × 2, `unified-transactions/cash`, `onboarding/bulk-create`, `cashAccount.ts` create) — refactoring them to use the helper is consistency churn without behaviour change; flagged as optional follow-up. **Out of scope** (audited but skipped): `prisma/seed-*.ts` (test fixtures, not load-bearing); `lib/testing/loader.ts` (test loader); `lib/db/tenant.ts` (multi-tenant scaffold not yet active). **Reviewer-reject rule** for future PRs: every new `prisma.account.{create, update, upsert}` that writes `currentBalance` MUST spread `...balanceWriteFields(source)`. The helper file's JSDoc states this in plain English; reviewers should cite it in code review. No schema change. No CDR posture change.

- **Phase 42 PR5.5 — PDF summary + receipt ZIP bundle SHIPPED (PR E, session 20).** Two new exporter modules under `lib/bookkeeping/taxPack/`: `pdfExporter.ts` (`buildTaxPackPdf(summary)` — `pdfkit`, A4, 6 sections — cover with FY label + generated date / totals / ATO labels table / per-property P&L blocks with sorted expense breakdowns / data-sources disclosure with per-source counts + month coverage / on-every-page footer disclaimer with horizontal rule) and `zipBundleBuilder.ts` (`buildReceiptZipBundle(userId, window)` — bundles every `Document` in the FY window where `category IN (RECEIPT, INVOICE, TAX)`; foldered by category — `Receipts/` / `Invoices/` / `Tax_Documents/` — with `<yyyy-MM-dd>_<originalFilename>` naming, dedup-counter for collisions, plain-text `MANIFEST.txt` with FY label + window dates + per-folder counts + missing count + disclaimer). **§12.7 dep decision:** added **only** `pdfkit` (one new dep, MIT, ~700KB, well-trodden Node PDF library — no GCP managed alternative exists for JSON-to-PDF generation); **dropped** the originally-planned `archiver` dep — reused the already-installed `jszip` instead, mirroring the canonical pattern at `/api/documents/export/route.ts`. Aggregate fetch failures are **non-blocking** — the granular table still renders, the 3 new columns just collapse out (the existing 5 columns stay). The product question raised in #9b — how much per-client aggregate to surface on the practice landing vs. behind the per-client drill-in (each drill-in writes a `ClientAccessLog` row but landing aggregates don't) — resolved by the architect lens: putting the aggregate on `/portal/clients` (the proper client-book surface) rather than the dashboard landing keeps the landing slim (the slim summary card from #9b stays) and keeps the per-client drill-in audit trail intact. Closes the §6b polish backlog (① + ② + ③ all shipped). No schema change. No new endpoint. No CDR posture change. tsc clean against existing patterns (`Map.get` + nullish-coalescing matches `aggregateMap` consumers elsewhere); Vercel preview is the canonical type check per §11.2.

- **feat(admin): `GET /api/admin/schema-drift` — server-side prod schema-drift audit (2026-05-12).** So the "audit prod for more pre-migration drift" item (checklist row 11 / Tech Debt #18) can be run with one click instead of setting up a local Prisma env with prod DB access. SUPER_ADMIN-only (`verifyAdminGCPAuth` + role check, mirrors `/api/admin/run-seed`). **Read-only** — runs only `SELECT`s against `information_schema` / `pg_catalog` over the live WIF connection, applies nothing. Compares `Prisma.dmmf` (what `schema.prisma` declares) vs prod's actual structure → JSON report: `missingTables`, `tablesWithMissingColumns` (each missing column with its Prisma type + a `suggestedAddColumnSql` *hint*), `tablesWithExtraColumns`, `missingEnums` / `enumsWithMissingValues`, `orphanTables`, and a `summary` with `hasDrift`. Scope: column/table/enum-value level (catches the `SELECT *`-crashes-on-a-missing-column class — the `basiq_connections.consentExpiresAt` bug); does NOT check types/nullability/defaults/indexes (for that, the local `prisma migrate diff`). Returns no data — only schema metadata (table/column/enum names). Falls back gracefully with a clear error if `Prisma.dmmf` isn't in the deployed bundle. Doc-sync: `04_PRISMA_MIGRATION_BASELINE.md` §12 (the endpoint added as option (a) alongside the local `prisma migrate diff` as option (b)) + the checklist row 11 + Tech Debt #18. **Reza-side: hit it, paste the JSON to Claude → Claude ships a corrective migration for any drift it finds.**

- **fix(db): corrective migration for `basiq_connections` pre-migration schema drift.** The `/api/cdr/lifecycle` cron (`monitrax-cdr-lifecycle` Cloud Scheduler job) was returning HTTP 500 on every run — root cause (via `curl -i`): `Invalid \`prisma.basiqConnection.findMany()\` invocation: The column \`basiq_connections.consentExpiresAt\` does not exist in the current database`. `BasiqConnection` in `schema.prisma` declares `consentExpiresAt DateTime?` + `consentScope String?` + a `consentExpiresAt` index (the "Fix: G19" CDR-consent-tracking columns), but these were added during the pre-migration `db push` era with no migration file — so prod's `basiq_connections` table never got them, and the first `SELECT *` on it (the cron's `findMany()` with no `select`) crashed. Fix-forward migration `prisma/migrations/20260514130000_fix_basiq_connection_consent_columns/migration.sql` — `ALTER TABLE "basiq_connections" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "consentExpiresAt" TIMESTAMP(3)` + `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "consentScope" TEXT` + `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ...`. Idempotent (`IF NOT EXISTS` → a no-op on dev where the columns already exist via `db push`, adds them on prod). Purely additive — §12.11 N/A. No `schema.prisma` change (the schema already declares the columns; this just brings prod in line). Doc-sync: `04_PRISMA_MIGRATION_BASELINE.md` §12 (new — "residual pre-migration drift, fix forward; run `prisma migrate diff` to find more") + Tech Debt #18 (audit prod schema vs `schema.prisma` for other drift, before the Basiq submission). **After this deploys: re-run the `monitrax-cdr-lifecycle` Cloud Scheduler job → should be 200.** (Also fixed in the same diagnostic session, Reza-side: the `monitrax-portal-alert-sweep` job was hitting `https://monitrax.com.au/...` — the apex domain, which 30x-redirects to `www.monitrax.com.au` and downgraded the POST→GET → HTTP 405 → updated the job's URL to `https://www.monitrax.com.au/api/portal/alerts/sweep`.)

New `engagement_state` table (one row per user) + pure `lib/bookkeeping/engagement/streak.ts` (deterministic state machine: NEW_STREAK / SAME_DAY / EXTENDED / SHIELD_USED / BROKEN_AND_RESTORED / BROKEN / RESET_AFTER_LONG_GAP) + `lib/bookkeeping/engagement/dailyPulse.ts` (composes period + engagement state + anomaly narrator; adaptive CTA).
