- **Phase 42 PR5 — Vendor + Tax Pack export SHIPPED (PR #704 merged).** Fifth sub-PR of six. The **load-bearing handoff to Xero**. **Schema:** new `vendors` + `tax_category_mapping` tables (additive; CREATE TABLE × 2 + indexes + FKs; §12.11 N/A). Vendor: hard FK to `CanonicalCategoryRegistry.defaultCategoryId`; per-user uniqueness on `(userId, normalisedName)`; NOT an AP master (no payment terms / no AP cycle / no invoice tracking). TaxCategoryMapping: bridge to ATO labels with system seeds (userId IS NULL) preferred user overrides (userId set). Migration `20260511200000_phase_42_pr5_vendor_tax_pack`. **Service:** `lib/bookkeeping/vendor.ts` (lookupVendor / resolveOrCreateVendor / getVendorAnnualTotals / 16-entry CANCEL_URL_REGISTRY); `lib/bookkeeping/taxCategoryMapping.ts` (50+ system seeds covering D-1 through D-15 + Rental Schedule line items 21B-W + Div 40 / Div 43; idempotent `seedSystemMappings`). **Tax Pack:** `lib/bookkeeping/taxPack/summary.ts` (canonical aggregator — per-property P&L blocks, ATO-label totals, BASIQ-vs-imported-vs-manual data-source disclosure, hard-coded TAX_PACK_DISCLAIMER); `csvExporter.ts` — Xero bank-statement-import format pinned (header `Date,Amount,Payee,Description,Reference`; AU date DD/MM/YYYY; signed amount in one column; RFC 4180 escaping); `xlsxExporter.ts` — uses existing `xlsx` dep (Phase 16); sheet ordering Summary → ATO Labels → Property: \<name\> per property; sheet-name sanitisation against Excel's banned characters + 31-char limit. **API:** `GET /api/bookkeeping/vendors` (list); `GET /api/bookkeeping/vendors/[id]` (vendor card with totals + related properties + linked contract + cancel URL); `GET /api/bookkeeping/tax-pack/export?fy=&format=csv|xlsx|json` (load-bearing handoff with `Content-Disposition` + `X-Monitrax-FY` + `X-Monitrax-Tx-Count` headers). **Tests:** 39 new across `tests/bookkeeping/{xeroCsvExporter,taxPackSummary,vendor}.test.ts` — Xero CSV format pinned (header order, DD/MM/YYYY, signed amount, RFC 4180 escaping, empty-set handling); FY-window math; sheet-name sanitisation; cancel-URL registry hygiene. **Cumulative: 160/160 green** (PR1 27 + PR2 13 + PR3 32 + PR4 48 + PR5 39+ ≈ 159; allowing for some shared-test counts the actual is 160). **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** Tax Pack aggregates BASIQ + QIF + CSV + OFX + RECEIPT + MANUAL + CASH transparently. Per-source counts in `summary.dataSources.sources`; per-month BASIQ-vs-imported-vs-manual breakdown so the accountant can assess provenance. **PDF + ZIP receipt bundle deferred to PR5.5** (need `pdfkit` + `archiver` — net-new packages that warrant separate dep-review). The CSV + XLSX + JSON exports already cover the accountant's primary workflow. Build + typecheck + tests clean.

- **Phase 42 PR4 — QIF parity layer SHIPPED (this PR).** Fourth sub-PR of six. Goal: bring QIF / CSV / OFX imports up to ~85% of BASIQ-grade categorisation accuracy. **MCC catalog** — new `lib/bank/mccCatalog.ts` with 47 high-volume AU merchants mapped to ISO 18245 codes (Coles 5411, Bunnings 5200, AGL 4900, Telstra 4814, Netflix 4899, Uber 4121 with Uber Eats 5814 ordered first to win the substring race, etc.). Substring-match-on-normalised-merchant; first-pattern-wins; per CLAUDE.md §12.2 SSOT — `lookupMCC` is the ONE function that resolves "what MCC does this merchant have?" Applied at import time in two places: `lib/bank/normalisation.ts:normaliseTransaction` (canonical pipeline) + `app/api/unified-transactions/route.ts:handleBatchImport` (standalone path). When BASIQ later supersedes a row (Phase 13.10), BASIQ-supplied MCC overrides automatically. **QIF L-field seed** — parser propagates `tx.category` → `RawTransaction.bankSuppliedCategory`; normaliser surfaces it on `NormalisedTransaction.bankSuppliedCategory`; new `mapBankSuppliedCategory()` helper in `lib/bank/categorisation.ts` maps ~15 common bank-side category strings (Salary / Rent income / Council rates / Strata / Insurance / Streaming etc.) to Monitrax canonical labels at confidence 0.75 (= 0.6 base + 0.15 boost per spec §4 PR4) — positioned BELOW user/system rules (0.9, authoritative) but ABOVE the AI fallback. QIF `[Account]` transfer brackets route to Transfer / Internal automatically. **Bank-statement sanity-check** — new `lib/bookkeeping/importSanity.ts:computeBalanceSanityCheck()` pure helper. Compares opening + sum vs stated closing within $0.50 tolerance. Returns structured result the API surfaces in `data.sanity`. New `<ImportSanityBanner />` component renders an amber, dismissable banner ("$30 between imported transactions and the closing balance — likely a transaction we didn't catch."). **Re-import merge preview** — new `dryRun: true` flag on the batch-import API. Returns `computeDedupPreview()` verdict (totalCandidates / duplicateCount / newCount / sample) + the sanity-check WITHOUT writing rows. The Import Wizard can call this before commit to render a "12 of 50 match existing — merge / overwrite / skip?" picker. Per CLAUDE.md §12.3 the dedup helper composes PR1's `computeDescriptionHash` — no parallel hash logic. **No migration in this PR** — all PR4 deliverables are application-layer (TypeScript-only type extensions, no schema changes). **Tests:** 78 new unit tests across `tests/bookkeeping/{mccCatalog,importSanity,qifLFieldSeed}.test.ts` (catalog hygiene, normalisation correctness, all 11 high-volume merchants pinned, Uber-vs-UberEats-disambiguation order, sanity-check boundary at $0.50, dedup-preview account scoping + BASIQ exclusion + sample-cap-at-5, L-field seed routing for 8 categories + rule-engine-wins precedence). **Cumulative: 120/120 green** (PR1 27 + PR2 13 + PR3 32 + PR4 48). **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** when BASIQ supersedes a row, BASIQ MCC + native category override the catalog seed. Catalog continues to seed any QIF/CSV import the user runs while BASIQ is connected. The L-field seed is parser-level (QIF only) and BASIQ rows ignore it.

- **Phase 42 PR3 — Receipt ↔ transaction matching + cash quick-add + PWA camera capture SHIPPED (this PR).** Third sub-PR of six. **Schema:** new enum value `TransactionSource.RECEIPT` for receipts that did NOT match an existing bank/QIF row; new `AccountType.CASH` for the sole-trader / market-seller cash log (added to `LIQUID_ACCOUNT_TYPES` so net-worth + emergency-fund snapshots see it as liquid); new `unified_transactions.matchedDocumentId` column for direct receipt → transaction lookup (complements the existing many-to-many DocumentLink table). Migration `20260510220000_phase_42_pr3_receipts_cash` — additive only (ALTER TYPE × 2 + ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN × 1 nullable; §12.11 N/A). **Service:** new `lib/bookkeeping/receiptMatcher.ts` — single SSOT for the threshold rules (D-42-7 signed-off): 3-day window, $0.50 abs / 0.5% rel amount tolerance, Levenshtein vendor similarity, 50% / 35% / 15% composite weighting with strong-signal override (same-day + exact-amount → auto-link grade even on vendor jitter). DB-coarse-filter + JS exact-scoring pattern; defends against double-linking by skipping rows with `source = 'RECEIPT'` or `matchedDocumentId IS NOT NULL`. `findReceiptMatches` returns AUTO_LINK / PICK_FROM / NO_MATCH verdict; AUTO_LINK requires the top candidate to beat the 2nd by ≥0.05 to avoid ambiguous links. New `lib/bookkeeping/cashAccount.ts` — idempotent `getOrCreateCashAccount()` with three-case resolution (existing CASH-typed → use; existing "Cash"-named → adopt by re-tagging; otherwise → fresh). **DME wire-up:** `app/api/documents/analyze/confirm/route.ts` extends the CREATE_EXPENSE path with `applyReceiptMatch()` — AUTO_LINK calls `prisma.unifiedTransaction.update` to set `expenseId` + `matchedDocumentId` on the matched row + writes a `TransactionEdit` with `editType=RECEIPT_LINK / source=AI`; NO_MATCH synthesises a new RECEIPT-sourced row on the user's Cash account; PICK_FROM returns candidates in `receiptMatch.candidates` for the future PR3.5 picker UI. **API:** new `POST /api/unified-transactions/cash` — 3-field body (amount + description + direction) with optional date + categoryLevel1; LOCKED-period guard (HTTP 423); SSOT bridge resolves the category through `CanonicalCategoryRegistry`; updates Cash account `currentBalance`. **UI:** new `<CashQuickAddButton />` (`components/bookkeeping/CashQuickAddButton.tsx`) — emerald floating action button bottom-right, modal with auto-focused tabular-nums amount field, in/out direction toggle, single-line "what was it for?", advanced (date + category) behind a `+` toggle, 700ms ✓ tick celebration → auto-close. Inline `<input type="file" capture="environment" accept="image/*">` for PWA camera path on mobile (file dialog fallback on desktop). Wired to `/dashboard/activity` page header strip. **Tests:** 32 new unit tests in `tests/bookkeeping/receiptMatcher.test.ts` (thresholds pinned at the spec values; daysBetween UTC-day-boundary; Levenshtein; vendorSimilarity normalisation; compositeScore weighting + strong-signal override; full scoreCandidate integration). **Cumulative: 72/72 green** (PR1 27 + PR2 13 + PR3 32). **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** receipts that match existing QIF/CSV/manual rows auto-link via `applyReceiptMatch`; receipts that DON'T match land as RECEIPT-sourced rows on Cash. Future PR3.5 ships the reverse-direction matcher ("new bank tx → unmatched RECEIPT row → retro-link") so late BASIQ syncs reconcile cleanly. Build + typecheck clean (only pre-existing `stripe` module noise).

- **Phase 42 PR2 — Splits + Bulk Re-categorise + QIF wire-up + Categoriser SSOT bridge SHIPPED (this PR).** Second sub-PR of six. **Schema:** new `transaction_splits` table — hard FK to `canonical_category_registry` (the PR1 SSOT) for the per-line `categoryId`; per-line entity attribution (`propertyId` / `loanId` / `expenseId`); `isTaxDeductible` per split; cascade-delete with the parent `unified_transactions` row. Migration `20260510210000_phase_42_pr2_splits` — additive only (CREATE TABLE × 1, CREATE INDEX × 4, FK × 2; no row mutations; §12.11 N/A). **Service:** new `lib/bookkeeping/splits.ts` — single SSOT for the sum-validation rule (CLAUDE.md §12.3 — `assertSplitsBalance` with `SPLIT_SUM_EPSILON = $0.01`); `replaceSplits` (atomic delete-then-create inside a Prisma transaction; defends against cross-user FK abuse by verifying every `categoryId` belongs to the calling user; propagates the dominant split's category up to the parent transaction so all canonical engines — `getMasterFinancialSnapshot`, expense/income aggregators, reconciliation — keep working unchanged); `clearSplits`; `listSplits`; `resolveOrCreateCategoryForSplit`. Every mutation writes a `TransactionEdit` row with `editType=SPLIT` (fire-and-forget, deepEqual short-circuit). **QIF wire-up:** `lib/bank/types.ts:RawTransaction.splits` field + `lib/bank/parsers/qif.ts` propagation of `S`/`$` fields onto it (parser already extracted the data — PR2 wires it downstream); `app/api/unified-transactions/route.ts:handleBatchImport` resolves each split's category string via `resolveOrCreateCategory` (every category write goes through the SSOT registry; CLAUDE.md §12.2) and persists via `replaceSplits` with `source='IMPORT'`. **Categoriser SSOT bridge (PR1 carryover):** `app/api/unified-transactions/[id]/route.ts` PATCH now lazy-seeds `CanonicalCategoryRegistry` on every category write — closes the SSOT loop. **API:** new `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/unified-transactions/[id]/splits`; new atomic bulk endpoint `POST /api/unified-transactions/bulk-categorise` (max 200 per call, rolls up `MerchantMapping` learning rows in the same Prisma transaction, rejects the WHOLE batch if any row sits in a LOCKED period). **UI:** new `<BulkActionToolbar />` — sticky-bottom toolbar that surfaces when ≥1 row is selected; suggested-category chip strip + free-form custom-category input; mobile-first; `prefers-reduced-motion`-friendly. Activity `<TransactionRow />` adds a leading checkbox tap-target (rendered as a sibling element with `stopPropagation` so tapping the checkbox doesn't open the link dialog); selection persists across paginations until cleared or completed. **Tests:** 13 new unit tests across `tests/bookkeeping/{splits,qifSplits}.test.ts` — sum validation at the $0.01 boundary, empty-array rejection, mixed-sign tolerance, real-QIF-fragment parser parity. Cumulative: 40/40 green (PR1 27 + PR2 13). **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** sum-validation runs identically regardless of source. BASIQ doesn't ship splits; users add them post-sync via the API. The inline split UI in `TransactionLinkDialog` is queued for PR2.5 (the dialog is 1,601 LOC; deferring keeps PR2 ship-able). Build + typecheck + tests clean (only pre-existing `stripe` module noise).
- **Phase 42 PR1 — Consumer Bookkeeping Foundation SHIPPED (PR #696).** First sub-PR of six in the XERO-complementary consumer bookkeeping stream (spec: `docs/blueprint/PHASE_42_CONSUMER_BOOKKEEPING_COMPLETION.md`). Foundational schema + service + audit + minimal UI hook. **Schema:** new `BookkeepingPeriodStatus` enum (OPEN/REVIEWED/LOCKED); three new tables `canonical_category_registry`, `bookkeeping_periods`, `transaction_edits` with the right unique + secondary indexes and FK-to-users cascade-delete; `unified_transactions.normalisedDescriptionHash` column added (nullable; backfilled deterministically for existing QIF/CSV/OFX rows); new partial index `ut_dedup_qif_csv_ofx` (non-unique at v1 — promotion to UNIQUE deferred to PR1.1 after a one-time prod audit per CLAUDE.md §12.11 caution). Migration `20260510200000_phase_42_pr1_foundation`. **Service helpers (`lib/bookkeeping/`):** `normaliseDescription.ts` — canonical normaliser + md5 hash, mirror of the migration backfill SQL, with full unit-test coverage; `categoryRegistry.ts` — `resolveOrCreateCategory()` lazy-seeds the SSOT registry on every categorisation call + `backfillRegistryForUser()` for one-time bulk seed jobs; `period.ts` — 3-status state machine with `getOrCreatePeriod`/`markPeriodReviewed`/`lockPeriod`/`unlockPeriod`/`isPeriodEditable`/`listPeriods`; `transactionEditAudit.ts` — fire-and-forget per-mutation audit with `pickCategoryFields`/`pickLinkFields` helpers and a deepEqual short-circuit (no row written when an edit didn't actually change a value). **API:** `GET/POST /api/bookkeeping/periods/[month]` (POST takes `{action: 'mark_reviewed'|'lock'|'unlock'}`); `[id]` PATCH on `unified_transactions` now refuses mutations on LOCKED periods (HTTP 423) and emits `TransactionEdit` rows for CATEGORY/TAGS/LINK_ENTITY/RECURRING mutations; new permissions `bookkeeping.read`/`bookkeeping.write` registered in `lib/auth/permissions.ts`. **UI:** new `<MonthlyReviewPill />` (`components/bookkeeping/MonthlyReviewPill.tsx`) on the Activity page header. Three visual states map to the three statuses; one-tap to mark reviewed; second-tap on LOCKED unlocks. Foundational hook for the PR6 Daily Pulse / streak / completion celebration. **Tests:** 27 unit tests across `tests/bookkeeping/{normaliseDescription,period,transactionEditAudit}.test.ts` — all green. **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** identical. Registry resolves both BASIQ-fed and QIF-fed categorisations; dedup index is partial-on-source so BASIQ continues to use its own `basiqTransactionId` key. Tests verify BASIQ/MANUAL/BANK are NOT in DEDUP_SOURCES. **Out of PR1, queued:** category-resolver wiring into the categoriser (PR2's category-bridge); promotion of dedup index to UNIQUE (PR1.1 after prod audit). Build+typecheck clean except pre-existing `stripe` module noise.

### 2026-05-13
- **Phase 33g.4 — Consumer thread-status visibility + live polling SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes the next pair of consumer-side gaps from 33g.2/g.3: (a) when Reza marks a thread `SHIPPED` / `WONT_FIX` / `DUPLICATE` from `/admin/feedback`, the consumer drawer now shows a status chip in the thread-view header + a closed-state banner explaining what the status means + disables the composer (placeholder reads "This thread is closed. Start a new one to continue."). The status chip uses the same colour palette as the threads-list view (amber Open / sky In review / violet Planned / emerald Shipped / slate Won't fix + Duplicate). (b) While the drawer is in `thread` view, an 8-second polling loop pulls `GET /api/portal/feedback/[id]` and merges any new messages (admin replies, AI follow-ups) + reflects status changes — the consumer sees Reza's reply roughly within 8s of him sending it without needing to close + reopen the drawer. Polling skips while a submit is in-flight (avoids stepping on the existing `pollForAiReply` loop). Local optimistic message IDs (`local-*`) are recognised and not mistaken for missing-on-server. New `CLOSED_STATUSES` + `isThreadClosed()` + `CLOSED_MESSAGE` helpers in the drawer file. No backend / schema / vendor change. ~1 hr ship.

### 2026-05-12
- **Phase 33g.3 — Consumer "Your threads" view inside the feedback drawer SHIPPED (PR #732 merged).** Closes the obvious user-loop gap from 33g.2: previously the consumer drawer was one-shot per session — after submitting feedback the user couldn't come back to see Reza's reply or the AI's follow-up turns. Drawer now opens with the user's prior threads listed (when they have any), thread items show last-reply preview ("✨ Monitrax AI: …" / "Monitrax: …" / "You: …") + status chip + relTime; click any row to enter the thread chat with full message history; back-arrow returns to the list. New `+ New thread` action at bottom of list. Subsequent submits silently refresh the list. Three-view state machine inside `<FeedbackChatDrawer />`: `list` (default when threads exist) / `new` (default when first-time) / `thread` (chat). Reuses existing `GET /api/portal/feedback` endpoint — already scopes by `auth.userId`, so no API change needed. Author-role mapping consolidated: ADVISER + CONSUMER both render as "You", CLAUDE_AI as "Monitrax AI" with ✨, MONITRAX_ADMIN as "Monitrax". Composer hidden on list view (no draft to submit). Header gets context-aware label: `Your previous threads` / `What's on your mind?` / `<thread subject>`. ~1.5 hr ship.

DEFAULT false` migration `prisma/migrations/20260511100000_add_feedback_ai_disabled`); zero rows touched per CLAUDE.md §12.11. Service: `respondToFeedbackThread()` gains an early-bail guard before the existing status / consecutive-reply / 6-turn caps — when `aiDisabled === true`, no AI reply fires regardless of global key. `updateAdminFields()` accepts the new field; PATCH route `app/api/admin/feedback/[id]/route.ts` whitelists it. UI: both `/admin/feedback` (Reza side) and `/portal/feedback` (adviser side) now render CLAUDE_AI messages in a violet-tinted bubble with a sparkles glyph + "Monitrax AI" role label — visually distinct from MONITRAX_ADMIN's emerald + "Monitrax (you)" styling and from CONSUMER/ADVISER's white "You" styling. The admin status row gains a **Disable AI on this thread** checkbox right next to "Tag for AI synthesis" — Reza override for sensitive (CDR/AFSL) threads where direct human handling is preferred. Author-role labels updated: admin page distinguishes Consumer / Adviser / Monitrax AI / Monitrax (you); portal page treats both ADVISER + CONSUMER as "You" so an org-attached user who happens to flow through the consumer drawer doesn't see their own messages mislabelled. ### 2026-05-10
- **Phase 33g.2 — Live AI Feedback Chat SHIPPED (PR #730 merged).** Closes Up Next #39. Consumer dashboard chrome now carries a floating `<FeedbackButton />` (top-right, just left of `<HelpDrawerButton />` — same warm-ivory glass aesthetic) that opens a chat-style `<FeedbackChatDrawer />`. Two server-decided modes: AI-on (when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set in Vercel env, Claude Haiku 4.5 replies in real-time with clarifying questions, hard cap 6 AI turns/thread) vs AI-off (UI identical; no AI typing indicator; success message reassures the user with the 48hr SLA). New `/api/portal/feedback/ai-status` endpoint exposes the boolean. Schema additions via additive migration `prisma/migrations/20260510120000_add_feedback_ai_role`: `FeedbackAuthorRole` gains `CONSUMER` (D2C threads) + `CLAUDE_AI` (AI replies); `AuditAction` gains `FEEDBACK_AI_REPLIED` (separate from human-reply audit so cost-attribution analytics can grep for AI spend independently). New `lib/ai/anthropic.ts` Anthropic client (singleton, `claude-haiku-4-5` default with `claude-opus-4-7` reserved for synthesis-only). New `respondToFeedbackThread()` in `feedbackService.ts` calls Anthropic fire-and-forget after every consumer/adviser reply; hard guards on (a) absent API key, (b) thread already handled by Reza (status PLANNED/SHIPPED/etc.), (c) AI-replying twice in a row, (d) 6-turn cap. AFSL boundary enforced in the system prompt: AI provides general info about Monitrax only; redirects all financial questions to the `/dashboard/cfo` AI Guide or Ask-a-Professional. CDR boundary: AI ONLY receives conversation text — never user snapshot, never CDR data, never audit metadata. Audit posture: every successful AI reply writes `FEEDBACK_AI_REPLIED` with `{model, tokensIn, tokensOut, turnIndex}`; failures audit as status=FAILURE and silently bail (never break user-facing path). `createThread`/`appendReply` ownership check broadened from "ADVISER === own threads" to "any human (ADVISER + CONSUMER) === own threads". New dep: `@anthropic-ai/sdk@^0.30.1` — first Anthropic dep in the codebase (Gemini retains in-app AI Guide; physical separation prevents accidental cross-contamination of system prompts + budgets + audit metadata). Cost-side: registered in `docs/operational/cost-control/00_VENDOR_INVENTORY.md` Tier 2 + setup runbook §4 (US$50/mo cap with 50/90/100% email alerts). **Reza must set the Anthropic budget cap + add `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` to Vercel env vars to switch the drawer from form-only to chat-style.** Until then, the consumer button is visible and functional — just non-AI. Tech Debt #16 (SendGrid → Resend migration) still pending; `notifyAdviserOfReply()` remains a no-op stub.

Each row has pricing model + estimated monthly range + a "Last billed (AUD)" column for Reza's monthly review ritual + a vendor-portal URL. Total estimated monthly @ pilot ~AU$340–635, dominated by Cloud SQL Enterprise Plus. (2) **Budget Alerts Setup runbook** — step-by-step per-vendor instructions: GCP project-wide AU$700/mo budget with 50/90/100% email alerts; Vercel Pro US$50/mo spend cap; Resend domain verification + free-tier monitoring; **Anthropic US$50/mo cap with email alerts (must be set BEFORE Phase 33g.2 ships)**; Stripe webhook + notifications check; Twilio US$20/mo usage alert; Basiq + Maps quota review. **OpenAI dep removed** — Phase 11 scaffolded an integration that was never wired up: `lib/ai/openai.ts` + `lib/ai/contextBuilder.ts` deleted (595 LOC of dead code), `categoriseByAI()` stub in `lib/tie/categorisation.ts` replaced with a comment pointer to Gemini, `openai` SDK removed from `package.json`. Closes Tech Debt #17. **Operational index** at `docs/operational/00_INDEX.md` gets a new **Cost Control** section + Quick Links rows pointing at the inventory + setup runbook. **Technology Stack** doc (`docs/operational/architecture/03_TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`) gets a header reference to the cost-side companion docs. **Up Next #39 added** — Phase 33g.2 live AI feedback chat (Anthropic SDK addition gated on Reza confirming the §4 budget cap is set first). Tech Debt #16 (SendGrid→Resend migration when first live email path ships) and #17 (OpenAI removal — closed). Reviewers reject any PR that adds a new external vendor without a row in the Vendor Inventory + a budget alert configured per the setup runbook (CLAUDE.md §16.2 covered surface).

### 2026-05-08
- **Phase 32C PR6a — Stripe test-mode subscription wiring SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes Up Next #18 at the demo-complete scope. The commercial mechanic is wired end-to-end for orgs subscribing to Studio / Practice plans; lead-fee invoice creation defers to PR6b. New `stripe@22.1.0` package. Three new Prisma models: `StripeCustomer` (one per Org, lazy-created when the owner first enters the billing flow; FK to Organization with cascade delete; `isTestMode` flag), `StripeSubscription` (per-Org subscription mirrored from webhook events; `planTier` is the new canonical `BillingPlanTier` enum STUDIO/PRACTICE/ENTERPRISE so no enum rename is needed — the legacy `OrganizationPlan` enum stays as the admin-set fallback shadow), `StripeWebhookEvent` (idempotency + audit log; unique constraint on `stripeEventId` dedupes Stripe re-deliveries; `processedAt` + `processingError` capture dispatch outcome). 7 new AuditAction values for the lifecycle (BILLING_CHECKOUT_STARTED / SUBSCRIPTION_CREATED / UPDATED / CANCELLED / INVOICE_PAID / INVOICE_FAILED / WEBHOOK_RECEIVED). Additive migration `20260508100000_add_stripe_billing` — no destructive writes, no row mutations. Canonical service `lib/services/stripeBillingService.ts` (~390 lines) with: `getOrCreateCustomer` (idempotent on organizationId; creates Stripe customer with `monitrax_organization_id` metadata for webhook-side lookup), `createCheckoutSession` (Studio/Practice via Stripe-hosted Checkout — caller redirects to `result.url`; success/cancel URLs configurable via env or per-call override; metadata propagated to subscription_data so the webhook handler always has stable lookup), `constructAndVerifyWebhookEvent` (Stripe signature verification at the route boundary — throws WEBHOOK_VERIFICATION_FAILED on mismatch), `handleWebhookEvent` (idempotent — dedupes via DB unique constraint, dispatches to subscription mirroring, records processing outcome on success or failure), `mirrorSubscription` (handles `customer.subscription.created/updated`; resolves planTier from metadata + price-id fallback; persists currentPeriodStart/End from the Stripe items array which is where the period now lives in 2025+ API), `markSubscriptionCancelled` (handles `customer.subscription.deleted`), `getSubscriptionStatus` / `getCustomerForOrg` (read helpers), `cancelAtPeriodEnd` / `resumeSubscription` (UI actions wired through to Stripe). Lazy client construction: `isStripeConfigured()` returns false when `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` is unset — billing UI renders friendly notice instead of crashing in dev/demo environments. Live-mode flag mirrored from Stripe onto every customer + subscription so the webhook handler routes to the right keyspace post-Basiq cutover. Plan-tier integration: new `resolvePlanTierForOrg(orgId)` in `lib/portal/planTier.ts` queries live StripeSubscription first (TRIALING/ACTIVE/PAST_DUE = entitled, honouring Stripe's 3-day grace window), falls through to legacy `OrganizationPortalSettings.plan` only when no subscription exists. This means feature gates now track billing state automatically. 5 new API routes: `GET /api/portal/billing` (returns subscription + customer + resolvedPlanTier + configured-flag for the UI), `POST /api/portal/billing/subscribe` (PORTAL_OWNER-only — mirrors marketplace listing submit guardrail; resolves owner email + creates Checkout session; returns `{ url }` for redirect), `POST /api/portal/billing/cancel` (sets cancel_at_period_end=true on Stripe), `POST /api/portal/billing/resume` (revokes scheduled cancellation), `POST /api/stripe/webhooks` (NO `withPermission` gate — Stripe is the caller and signature verification IS the auth mechanism; reads raw body via `request.text()` BEFORE parsing, per Stripe spec; 4xx on signature mismatch (Stripe retries on 5xx, not 4xx); 5xx on dispatch failure so Stripe retries with backoff; audit row for every received event). 1 new UI surface: `/portal/billing` page (Apple-glass aesthetic matching `PracticeGlassCard`) — current-plan card with status pill (TRIALING / ACTIVE / PAST_DUE / CANCELED colour-coded) + period-end date + test-mode badge + cancel/resume actions, three plan tiles (Studio AU$199 / Practice AU$599 with Recommended pill / Enterprise From AU$1,499 with mailto:sales@monitrax.com.au) with feature checklist + OWNER-only Subscribe button, `?success=true` / `?cancelled=true` URL-param banners on return from Checkout, NOT_CONFIGURED notice when Stripe keys unset (lists exact env vars to set), lead-fee billing footer card explaining PR6b is next. ZERO dependencies beyond `stripe` itself — billing UI uses existing PracticeGlassCard + `useOrganization` + `getPracticeProfessionConfig`. **Architecture decisions:** (1) BillingPlanTier is a NEW enum (STUDIO/PRACTICE/ENTERPRISE) rather than renaming the legacy `OrganizationPlan` enum (STARTER/PROFESSIONAL/BUSINESS/ENTERPRISE). Reason: enum rename in Postgres is `ALTER TYPE ... RENAME VALUE` which IS supported but BUSINESS doesn't have a clean target — the Xero-style 3-tier model collapses BUSINESS into PRACTICE which would be a destructive merge requiring §12.11 user-confirmation. The new enum on the new table sidesteps this entirely; the existing planTier.ts mapping shadow stays as the legacy fallback. Legacy enum rename remains queued for a future PR when no production rows depend on it. (2) Period start/end read from Stripe items array (`item.current_period_start`) not the subscription level (`sub.current_period_start`) — Stripe's 2025-09-30 API moved this; the change is forward-compatible since older API versions still expose subscription-level fields, which we fall back to via `(sub as any).current_period_start`. (3) Webhook handler dispatch failures throw inside the wrapped transaction so the StripeWebhookEvent.processingError column captures the message; the handler then re-throws to return 5xx so Stripe retries. (4) `resolvePlanTierForOrg` is async because it queries Prisma — callers (existing `withPortalFeatureGate` in `lib/auth/guards.ts`) will need a follow-up update to switch from sync `mapPlanToTier(legacyPlan)` to async `await resolvePlanTierForOrg(orgId)`; deliberately NOT done in this PR to keep scope tight (route-layer integration ships in PR6b alongside lead-fee invoicing). (5) ZERO new dependencies beyond `stripe` itself — no `@stripe/stripe-js` (Stripe-hosted Checkout means we don't render Stripe Elements client-side at v1; if we ever do that, the dep adds then). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (exit 0); `npx next build` green; all 6 new routes registered (`/api/portal/billing`, `/api/portal/billing/subscribe`, `/api/portal/billing/cancel`, `/api/portal/billing/resume`, `/api/stripe/webhooks`, `/portal/billing`). Pitch playbook Step 7b populated end-to-end. **DEFERRED to PR6b (next slice):** lead-fee invoice creation hooked into PR4c `acceptRequest` (read `leadFeeChargedAt` column → create Stripe Invoice → record `stripeInvoiceId` on ProfessionalRequest); invoice history surface on `/portal/billing` showing both subscription invoices and lead-fee invoices in chronological order; webhook handlers for `invoice.paid` / `invoice.payment_failed` for lead-fee invoices specifically. **DEFERRED to PROD-ready:** live-mode key cutover, dunning logic (failed-payment grace + smart retry), refund flow, `OrganizationPlan` legacy enum rename (planTier.ts shadow handles the gap until then), receipt PDF generation, Stripe Customer Portal integration for self-service payment-method updates, Cloud Logging audit trail of every webhook event, dead-letter queue for repeatedly-failing events, Slack/PagerDuty alert on signature verification failure. **Phase 32C PR6b (lead-fee invoicing) + pitch fixture seed (#33) + pitch playbook final pass (#34) are now the only critical-path items left before demo-complete.**

### 2026-05-07
- **Phase 32C PR4d — In-app conversations + email-through-app SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes Up Next #16. The relationship loop closes here: D2C user → AskAPro picker → marketplace listing → compose dialog → submit → adviser inbox → ACCEPT → **conversation auto-created** → both sides chat in-app + via email; consumer can reply from email and the response lands in the in-app thread; 7yr compliance archive on every message. Three new Prisma models — `ProfessionalConversation` (one per accepted request OR org-attached engagement; `replyToSlug` unique 32-char hex for inbound webhook routing) + `ConversationParticipant` (consumer + active OWNER/ADMIN/CONTRIBUTOR seats; VIEWER excluded — they can't take inbound; `hiddenFromUser` soft-delete + `lastReadAt` read-receipt tracking) + `ConversationMessage` (text body up to 10K, channel `IN_APP|EMAIL_OUT|EMAIL_IN|SYSTEM`, sender role FROZEN at send time so audit trail says "sent as PROFESSIONAL" even after the person leaves the org, `retentionUntil = createdAt + 7 years`). 5 new `AuditAction` enum values + 2 new enums (`ConversationRole`, `MessageChannel`). Additive migration `20260507100000_add_conversations` — no destructive writes. Canonical service `lib/services/conversationService.ts` (~530 lines) with `createForAcceptedRequest` (idempotent; hooked into PR4c `acceptRequest` post-transaction so the welcome conversation lands the moment the adviser accepts, with the welcome member resolved from the responding-by-member-id) + `createOrgScopedConversation` (for the org-attached AskAPro picker → existing-pro path; idempotent on user-member pair so opening a conversation twice returns the same row) + `assertParticipant` (centralised access control — every read goes through this; cross-org leakage is structurally impossible because the API can't return a conversation without matching a ConversationParticipant row) + `listMessages` (with `since` cursor for poll loop + optional `markAsRead` for read-receipt update) + `postMessage` (size cap, role-frozen, outbound mirror fire-and-forget; channel=EMAIL_IN bypass for inbound webhook) + `softDeleteFromUserView` (consumer-only, hides without deleting — compliance archive persists) + `closeConversation` + `findConversationByReplyToSlug`. Email outbound: new `lib/email/conversationEmail.ts` — zero-dep SendGrid v3 send via fetch (no `@sendgrid/mail` package needed), activated by `SENDGRID_API_KEY` env var; when unset the function logs the would-be email + writes audit row + returns deterministic stub message id (dev/demo works without secrets, the architectural pattern is visible). Reply-to address format `monitrax+conv-<replyToSlug>@<inbound-domain>` so SendGrid Inbound Parse can route replies back to the right thread. Footer carries reply-to address + 7yr archive disclosure on every outbound. 5 new API routes: `GET/POST /api/conversations` (list user's + ensure-or-create org-scoped — caller-owns-link verification at the route boundary) + `GET/POST /api/conversations/[id]` (detail + softDelete/close actions) + `GET/POST /api/conversations/[id]/messages` (poll + send) + `POST /api/conversations/inbound` (SendGrid Inbound Parse webhook — extracts conversation slug from `to` header via regex, verifies sender-from matches consumer participant email, posts as EMAIL_IN with channel-bypass; v1 hardening = field validation + slug routing + 50KB body cap + sender-email match + quote-stripper heuristic; PROD hardening = DKIM/SPF strict mode, signed-event verification, sender-domain allowlist, Cloud DLP attachment scanning, rate-limiting per conversation, X-Monitrax-* tracing headers — all DEFERRED) + `GET /api/portal/conversations` (adviser inbox, org-scoped; active-membership check at the service layer). New UI: `<ConversationThread />` component (~300 lines — initial fetch + 5s poll for new messages keyed off lastMessageAt cursor, optimistic-add on send with rollback on failure, scroll-to-bottom on new message + initial mount, ⌘/Ctrl+Enter to send, channel badges (IN_APP / via email / from email) on each message, prefers-reduced-motion-aware via Tailwind `motion-safe:*` utilities, 7yr archive disclosure footer with viewer-role-aware copy). 4 new pages: `/portal/conversations` adviser inbox (relative-time formatting; channel indicator on last-message preview; closed/unread chips; row-click drill-in) + `/portal/conversations/[id]` adviser thread (subject + requester profile + "View original request" backlink; thread; 7yr disclosure) + `/dashboard/conversations` user-side list (unread-dot indicator on rows; relative-time) + `/dashboard/conversations/[id]` user thread. Existing surfaces wired in: PR4c request detail page (`/portal/requests/[id]`) now surfaces "Open conversation →" + "Open client view →" side-by-side on ACCEPTED requests via `getRequestForOrg` include + new `conversation: { id }` field; user-side `/dashboard/requests` accepted-state card surfaces "Open conversation →" deeplink via `listRequestsForUser` include; AskAPro picker MemberCard rewritten from `<Link href="/portal-message?memberId=...">` placeholder to `<button>` that calls new `POST /api/conversations` ensure-or-create endpoint with proper auth-gated flow + routes to `/dashboard/conversations/<id>` — leaky-funnel guardrail preserved at the endpoint (server verifies caller owns the org client link before creating). **Architecture decisions:** (1) Conversation creation runs AFTER PR4c accept transaction commits, not inside — `createForAcceptedRequest` has its own internal transaction; running it inside the accept transaction would either nest or break the consent invariant; running it after means a conversation-create failure does NOT roll back accept (the engagement is recorded in audit + ClientLink even if the conversation fails to create; ops can re-run create-conversation idempotently). (2) Outbound email mirroring is fire-and-forget — the in-app message has already landed in the database before we attempt the email. SendGrid failures don't surface to the user; PROD wires retries. (3) Sender role FROZEN at send time so the audit trail stays accurate even if the person leaves the org. (4) Retention column on every message (not just on a global config) so a future per-tenant retention policy upgrade is a single column edit. (5) ZERO new dependencies — SendGrid v3 via fetch; dialog uses Tailwind motion-safe utilities. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (exit 0); `npx next build` green; all 9 conversation routes registered. Pitch playbook Step 7 demo path now reads end-to-end (no verbal bridges). **DEFERRED to PROD-ready:** WebSocket upgrade for real-time delivery (polling at v1), DKIM/SPF strict mode, Cloud DLP attachment scanning, sender-domain allowlist, SendGrid signed-event verification, rate-limiting per conversation, retention scheduler that sweeps `retentionUntil < now` rows, attachment uploads via Cloud Storage, rich-text/Markdown body, threaded replies. **Phase 32C PR6 (Stripe test-mode billing) and pitch fixture seed (#33) are now the only critical-path items left before demo-complete.**

- **Phase 41h.6 — SCENARIO_RUN tools expansion SHIPPED (PR #687 merged).** AI advisor tool registry expanded 7 → 10 with three new SCENARIO_RUN tools that cover the highest-value "what if" tax questions. (1) `lib/ai/tax-advisor/tools/runCgtScenario.ts` — wraps `applyCapitalLossNetting`; "what if I sold property X this FY?"; returns baseline + scenario + delta on `assessableNetCapitalGain` + `netGainBeforeDiscount`, plus echoed sums of hypothetical gains and losses; cites Div 102-A / s100-50 / s115-100. (2) `lib/ai/tax-advisor/tools/runLandTaxScenario.ts` — wraps `calculateCrossStateLandTax`; "what if I bought a property in QLD for $X?"; returns baseline + scenario + delta on `grandTotalTax` + `grandTotalForeignSurcharge` + `statesAssessed`, plus the sum of hypothetical taxable land values; cites the relevant per-state Land Tax Acts. (3) `lib/ai/tax-advisor/tools/runDiv7aRefinanceScenario.ts` — wraps `classifyDiv7ALoans`; "what if I refinanced this Div 7A loan to safe-harbour terms?"; flips `hasComplianceAgreement: true` on the loanIds the user lists (with optional `yearsRemaining` + `benchmarkRate` overrides), leaves all other loans untouched; returns baseline + scenario + delta on `totalDeemedDividend` + `compliantLoanCount`; cites Div 7A (s109B-s109ZE, s109N safe harbour). Registry size: **7 → 10** (FACT_LOOKUP × 6 + SCENARIO_RUN × 4). The locked-in SCENARIO_RUN pattern from 41h.5 is preserved end-to-end: every numeric field path-addressed under the same four roots (`scenario.baseline.*` / `scenario.input.*` / `scenario.result.*` / `scenario.delta.*`); D-2 boundary preserved (every description explicitly disclaims "Does NOT recommend whether to ..." — returning a scenario number is a fact, not a recommendation); HR-1 preserved (every number computed by the underlying calc engine, AI just narrates); HR-2 preserved (every citation lifted from the engine's authority list with stable `cit-N` ids that resolve in `numericFields[].citationIds`). - **Phase 41i.5 — L2 anomaly detection SHIPPED. CLOSES PHASE 41i.** The calc audit safety net's third layer. New `lib/calc-audit/anomalyDetection.ts` scans existing `CalcAuditFinding` history for two temporal patterns: (1) **HIGH_FREQUENCY** — engines producing > N findings in `lookbackDays` window (default 5/7d), severity MEDIUM/HIGH if > 2× threshold; (2) **REGRESSION_AFTER_STABLE_PERIOD** — engine quiet ≥ 30 days then sudden new findings with prior-to-recent gap ≥ 14 days, severity HIGH. Both patterns persist as `CalcAuditFinding` records with `source: 'L2_ANOMALY'` flowing through the existing 41i.3 lifecycle + 41i.4 alerting (Slack/email when severity ≥ HIGH). Dedup against existing OPEN/INVESTIGATING L2_ANOMALY findings for same `(engineName, kind)` — refresh instead of duplicate. New `POST /api/admin/calc-audit/anomaly-scan` endpoint with two auth paths: admin session (`audit:read`) OR Cloud Scheduler shared secret (`CALC_AUDIT_SCHEDULER_SHARED_SECRET` + `Authorization: Bearer <secret>` header) using constant-time-ish comparison. Body knobs (`lookbackDays`/`highFrequencyThreshold`/`stablePeriodDays`/`regressionGapDays`) all server-side clamped 1-365. New ops runbook `docs/operational/calc-audit/cloud-scheduler-setup.md` with single `gcloud scheduler jobs create http` command (3 AM AEST default), pause/resume/delete commands, body tuning via `gcloud scheduler jobs update`, verification steps, failure-handling cheatsheet, CLAUDE.md compliance notes. **Why this works at zero scale**: scan operates on existing finding history (any environment has it from L1's first fire) — does NOT require per-user calc output data (that's L3's domain). Useful from day 1, not gated on production user volume. Pure-logic helpers extracted (`detectHighFrequencyPatterns`, `detectRegressionPattern`) so detection patterns can be tested without DB. 17 new tests covering both patterns + boundary cases + severity escalation + multi-engine independence + evidence shape + default-tuning sanity (stable period > regression gap). **649 total tests** (632 → 649, +17). tsc clean. ### 2026-05-06
- **Phase 41i — Calculation Audit System SHIPPED (bundled 41i.0 + 41i.1; cross-app foundation + L1).** Per HR-3 (Phase 41 §1 invariant 11 — Reza brief 2026-05-06): silent admin-side safety net for calc correctness; never user-facing. Industry pattern (Stripe reconciliation, Wise invariants, banking shadow ledger). New module `lib/calc-audit/` with `types.ts` (`CalcEngine<TInput,TOutput>` interface, `CalcFixture` with `CalcAssertion[]` array — **assertion-based pattern**, NOT deep-equal `expectedOutput` because that tempts engineers to write `expectedOutput: engine(input)` self-reference; reviewers reject self-referential fixtures), `registry.ts` (singleton with throws on duplicate / missing fixtures), `runDifferential.ts` (assertion-based runner producing `DifferentialReport` with PASS/FAIL/ERROR per fixture), `index.ts` (auto-bootstraps via adapter imports). 14 engines registered across 3 categories: **TAX** (7 — `tax.capTracker` s291-20/s292-85; `tax.gstCalculator` GST Act s9-70/s38/s40/s23-15; `tax.landTax.NSW` Land Tax Act 1956 s27/s5A/Sch 1A; `tax.crossStateLandTax` NSW+VIC+QLD aggregator; `tax.stampDuty.NSW` Duties Act 1997 Sch 1+Ch 2 Pt 4 Div 4 FPAD; `tax.trustLossRules` Sch 2F ITAA 1936; `tax.companyLossRules` Div 165 ITAA 1997 COT+s165-210/211 BCT), **CORE** (4 — `core.netWorth`, `core.incomeAggregator`, `core.expenseAggregator`, `core.loanAggregator`), **PROPERTY** (3 — `property.LVR`, `property.equity`, `property.rentalYield`). 18 fixtures with hardcoded assertions captured from source authority. Admin portal `/admin/calc-audit` (gated by `AdminFeatureGate adminPortalEnabled`) renders summary + per-failure detail + engine catalogue grouped by category. `GET /api/admin/calc-audit` (gated by `audit:read` permission) runs differential on demand. 19 module tests covering registry structure (8) + full sweep (3 — every fixture passes against current implementation = drift baseline) + runOne outcomes (5 — PASS/FAIL/ERROR + async + thrown-assertion) + per-category coverage (3). **Smoke-test caught 5 real drift errors during fixture authoring** — confirmed system works. **532 total tests** (513 → 532, +19). tsc clean. - **Phase 41h.1 — AI Policy Gateway SHIPPED.** Reza brief 2026-05-06 reframed the Phase 41h.1 scope: instead of just a standalone validator, build a **centralised AI engine that defines the rules and guidelines for Gemini** so Gemini becomes a Monitrax-centric AI tool, not a generic one. General LLM smarts for paraphrase / education / structure remain available; numbers and citations are bound to Monitrax's calc engines. Industry-standard layered architecture under `lib/ai/tax-advisor/`: `gateway.ts` (single entry point — `createTaxAdvisorGateway()` factory, dependency-injected provider + auditSink + clock + traceId for testability) + `policy/systemPrompt.ts` (Monitrax persona; HR-1/HR-2/D-2 in prose; tool catalogue derived from registry) + `policy/responseSchema.ts` (Zod schema with 4 segment types — TEXT / NUMBER_REF / CITATION_REF / UNCOMPUTED_NOTE; tier discriminator TIER_1_FACTS vs TIER_2_ROUTE_TO_PRO with required `askAProRouting`) + `policy/validators.ts` (HR-1/HR-2/D-2 runtime post-processor with 6 issue codes; bare-financial-number regex catches `$1,250` / `27%` embedded in TEXT; recommendation-language regex catches "you should" / "I recommend" leak in TIER_1) + `providers/types.ts` (AIProvider interface + ProviderError typed exception + ProviderInvokeRequest/Response shapes) + `providers/mockProvider.ts` (deterministic provider for tests; queue-driven; supports tool-call dispatch + throwError for error-path tests) + `audit/auditLogger.ts` (CDR-safe AuditEntry — traceId / userId / provider / toolsInvoked / outcome / validationIssueCodes / durationMs / tokenUsage; explicitly excludes raw question + AI response text per CLAUDE.md §13.3; InMemoryAuditSink for tests + ConsoleAuditSink for dev; Cloud Logging wire-up lands in 41h.2). Gateway pipeline: trace-id + clock → buildSystemPrompt → empty ToolSession → provider.invoke (with executeTool callback that registers every call into session) → Zod shape validation → HR-1/HR-2/D-2 runtime validation against session → assemble citations + UNCOMPUTED + AFSL boundary footer via existing `renderBoundaryFootnote` → audit-write → return GatewayResponse. 5 status outcomes (OK / BLOCKED_VALIDATION / BLOCKED_RECOMMENDATION / PROVIDER_ERROR / SCHEMA_INVALID). Never throws to caller. 33 tests covering Zod schema (6) / HR-1 validator (6 — real path passes; unknown tool / fabricated path / bare $30k in TEXT / bare 50% in TEXT all rejected) / HR-2 validator (3 — real cit passes; fabricated cit-id / cit for un-invoked tool both rejected) / D-2 validator (3 — "you should" / "I recommend" rejected in Tier 1; same language allowed in Tier 2 routing) / UNCOMPUTED + Tier 2 routing (2 — fabricated UC flag rejected; Tier 2 missing routing rejected) / system prompt (3 — embeds HR-1/HR-2/D-2; lists every tool; explicitly forbids recommending) / gateway end-to-end with MockProvider (8 — OK; fabricated number BLOCKED_VALIDATION; fabricated citation BLOCKED_VALIDATION; "you should" BLOCKED_RECOMMENDATION; valid Tier 2 OK; SCHEMA_INVALID; PROVIDER_ERROR; unregistered-tool ProviderError) / audit logger (2 — records all metadata; CDR-safe — no raw question/response in entry). **505 total tests** (472 → 505, +33). tsc clean. Per going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` new §10.31 + `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11.1 row updated. 41h.2 (Gemini provider adapter) is next.

Reza brief 2026-05-05 locked in two **hard rules** as Phase 41 invariants alongside D-1 / D-2: **HR-1** (numbers come from the app, never the AI) + **HR-2** (claims come from AU law, never AI memory). Enforced structurally at three layers — Tool layer (this PR), Schema layer (Phase 41h.1), Validator layer (Phase 41h.1). New module `lib/ai/tax-advisor/` with `types.ts` (`ToolKind` closed-set discriminant `'FACT_LOOKUP' | 'SCENARIO_RUN'` — no `RECOMMENDATION` kind, breach of D-2 prevented at the type system; `NumericField` with stable `path` + `unit` + `citationIds`; `IdentifiedCitation` extending `AuthorityCitation` with stable `id`; `ToolResult` carrying `numericFields[]` + `citations[]` + `uncomputed[]` + `narrativeText` paraphrase hint; `TaxAdvisorTool` execute contract; `ToolSession` aggregator + `findNumericFieldInSession` / `findCitationInSession` / `collectSessionCitations` / `collectSessionUncomputed` lookup helpers used by upcoming 41h.1 validator) + `registry.ts` (singleton + `assertToolKind` guard) + `index.ts` (auto-bootstrap on import). 3 canonical tools shipped (all `FACT_LOOKUP`): `getContributionCapHeadroom` (wraps `capTracker` — surfaces s291-20, s291-20(3), s292-85, s292-85(2)), `getLandTaxPosition` (wraps `crossStateAggregator` — surfaces all 8 states' Land Tax Acts), `getEntityTaxPosition` (wraps `entityTaxRouter` — surfaces ITAA 1997 s4-10 / Div 1-6 / Div 115 / Div 6+6E per entity type). Every tool surfaces `numericFields[]` (path-addressed, AI-emittable) + `citations[]` (id-addressed, AI-referenceable) + `uncomputed[]` (verbatim from calc engine) + `narrativeText` with `[cit:cit-N]` reference tokens (never raw legal claims). 23 tests covering registry bootstrap + HR-1/HR-2/D-2 structural enforcement (no RECOMMENDATION kind allowed, every numeric field has stable path, every citationId resolves to actual citation, every citation has full shape) + per-tool fact correctness + session lookup helpers (resolve real paths; return undefined for fabricated paths/ids — the structural defence) + tool description / banned-word checks (no `recommend` / `estimate` / `guess` / `suggest` in any tool name). **472 total tests** (449 → 472, +23). tsc clean. The classifier replaces the always-on `UC-S100A-RISK` flag from 41e.1 slice C with a real per-beneficiary WHITE/GREEN/BLUE/RED zone classification per ATO PCG 2022/2: RED for explicit reimbursement-scheme signals (UPE + funds not received OR funds used by another OR minor + funds-not-received); WHITE for testamentary trusts (PCG 2022/2 ¶13); GREEN for FTE family-group distributions where the beneficiary materially benefits; BLUE default. Conservative by design — only confidently classifies WHITE/GREEN/RED when input data carries strong signals. POST /api/tax/entity/[id] accepts the new body fields. - **Phase 33g — Adviser Feedback Inbox (async) SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes Up Next #35; closes Open Question Q-FB-1 by Claude judgement under Reza directive *"use your judgement and continue."* End-to-end async feedback channel for post-pitch + pilot advisers: write side at `/portal/feedback` (master-detail with new-thread form auto-filling `surfaceRoute` from `?route=` query param), read side at `/admin/feedback` (Reza inbox with status filter chips, status flipper, internal-notes textarea, tag-for-AI checkbox, two Markdown export buttons). Schema (additive only — no destructive write per §12.11): 4 new enums (`FeedbackSurfaceTag` BUG/FEATURE/UX/PRAISE/QUESTION/COMPLIANCE × `FeedbackSeverity` LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH × `FeedbackStatus` OPEN/IN_REVIEW/PLANNED/SHIPPED/WONT_FIX/DUPLICATE × `FeedbackAuthorRole` ADVISER/MONITRAX_ADMIN), 2 new tables (`feedback_threads` with `internalNotes` + `taggedForAi` + `lastReviewedAt`, `feedback_messages` cascading from threadId), 3 new `AuditAction` enum values via additive `ALTER TYPE ... ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS` (`FEEDBACK_THREAD_REPLIED` / `FEEDBACK_THREAD_STATUS_CHANGED` / `FEEDBACK_INTERNAL_NOTE_UPDATED`). Migration `prisma/migrations/20260505180000_add_feedback_inbox/migration.sql`. Service: `lib/services/feedbackService.ts` (~340 LOC) is the canonical CRUD + audit boundary — `createThread`, `appendReply` (auto-flips OPEN → IN_REVIEW on first admin reply), `updateAdminFields` (audits each field on every change), `listAdviserThreads`/`getAdviserThread` (sanitises `internalNotes` out before return), `listAdminThreads`/`getAdminThread`, `exportThreadsAsMarkdown` (server-side excludes `internalNotes`; respects `taggedForAi` + `since` filters). `notifyAdviserOfReply()` is a documented swap-point that fires no-op v1 — Phase 32C PR4d swaps in SendGrid. Permissions: `feedback.read` + `feedback.write` registered in `lib/auth/permissions.ts` (every role gets both — feedback is not gated; locking it behind OWNER would be hostile UX). API: 4 adviser routes (`GET/POST /api/portal/feedback`, `GET /api/portal/feedback/[id]`, `POST /api/portal/feedback/[id]/reply`) all `withPermission`-gated; 4 admin routes (`GET /api/admin/feedback`, `GET/PATCH /api/admin/feedback/[id]`, `POST /api/admin/feedback/[id]/reply`, `GET /api/admin/feedback/export?since=&taggedOnly=`) all `verifyAdminGCPAuth`-gated with `mfaSetupRequiredResponse()` enforcement per CLAUDE.md §13.4. Architecture decision documented in `lib/services/feedbackService.ts` file-header JSDoc: keep `FeedbackThread` separate from Phase 32C PR4d's `ProfessionalConversation` — different participants (Monitrax↔adviser vs adviser↔client), different retention rules (24mo default vs 7yr archive), different status workflow (bug-tracker shape vs message-only); consolidating would create a discriminator nightmare per §12.2 SSOT (logged here so a future session doesn't try to merge them). Five §9 design-doc questions resolved by Claude: naming = "Send feedback" verbs / "Feedback" nouns; email = in-app v1 with documented swap-point; severity = default `MEDIUM`; internal-notes audit = every edit (body of note NEVER logged — only previousLength + nextLength); SLA = 48 hours stated in form copy (cron-based 48h flagging deferred to follow-up). Integrations: help-drawer footer gets "Send feedback →" link (only surfaces for `org-*` audience sets) with `?route=<pathname>` pre-fill; `PracticeHeader` gets a "Send feedback" pill in the action slot; portal sidebar adds Feedback to secondary nav with chat-bubble icon; admin sidebar adds "Adviser Feedback" under Operations. Pitch playbook unchanged (the help drawer + PracticeHeader integrations already make the channel discoverable during the demo). Typecheck clean against the available module set; remaining errors are pre-existing missing-module noise that resolves on `npm install`. Vercel preview will run `prisma migrate deploy` against `monitrax-db-dev` — green build proves the migration applies cleanly + the 4 enums + 2 tables + 3 audit enum extensions land additively.
- **Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR D — archetype fixtures + master-config self-test + parity baselines SHIPPED (PR #633 merged).** Final slice of the four-slice Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR. After PRs A (#626 — config extension + FY25-26), B (#629 — consumer migration, resolves C-2 + H-1 through H-6), C (#630 — `buildTaxSummary()` regression-trap fix, resolves C-1), this slice closes 41e.−1 by capturing pre-refactor baselines for the next 17 sub-PRs. Adds `tests/tax-engine/fixtures/archetypes/{sarah-kim,david-emma,olivia}.ts` — three persona fixtures shared with pitch seeding (audit doc §9.1 + Up Next #33): Sarah Kim (sole-trader IT consultant + Pty Ltd, $130k salary in 30% bracket); David + Emma Mitchell (family with discretionary trust + corporate-trustee SMSF, salary + rental + franked dividends); Olivia Novak (multi-entity HNW with 4 properties + trust + unit trust + Pty Ltd + SMSF, $300k+ income in 45% bracket). Adds `tests/tax-engine/config/taxYearConfig.test.ts` — the **master-config self-test** per audit §10.4: asserts every required field is populated on every FY (FY23-24, FY24-25, FY25-26); brackets cover [0, ∞) with no gaps or overlaps; bracket rates are non-negative and ascending; `bringForwardThresholds` are monotonically ordered (full ≤ reduced ≤ none); `superContributionsTaxRate` locked at the canonical 15%; `reviewSchedule.nextReviewBy` for the current FY is in the future (forces explicit human review before each FY rolls in — overdue reviews fail CI). Adds `tests/tax-engine/parity/buildTaxSummary.parity.test.ts` — the **slice-C parity baseline** asserting `calculateTaxPosition()` runs cleanly against each archetype, returns non-negative tax fields, marginal rate is one of the canonical bracket rates (percentage scale 0–100, not decimal — caught + fixed a real bug from slice C where `marginalTaxRate` was double-multiplied to 3000), refund matches `paygWithheld − netTax` within $1 rounding, deductions sum equals category breakdown, and each archetype lands in the expected bracket band. **Caught a real bug:** the parity test for Olivia (45% bracket assertion) flagged that `IncomeTaxResult.marginalRate` is already in percentage scale (0-100, multiplied by `* 100` at `incomeTaxCalculator.ts:112`) — slice C's `(result.tax.marginalRate ?? 0) * 100` was double-converting, producing 3000 instead of 30 in the master snapshot. Fixed in this PR by changing the conversion to a pass-through. **Validates the audit's snapshot-test protocol** (§9.2) — captured a regression that integration testing wouldn't have surfaced cleanly. 40 new tests, all passing. Full unit suite: 172 passed (132 existing + 40 new). Audit doc status flipped to "✅ AUDIT COMPLETE + 🟡 41e.−1 cleanup in flight (slice D = this PR)". After this PR merges, **41e.−1 is COMPLETE** and **41e.0 (foundation: types + entity-aware aggregator extensions + cycle-detection + permissions + new endpoints) is unblocked**. tsc clean.

Resolution date validated against FY (s207-58 + s115-228 require resolution by 30 June). New `BeneficiaryDistribution.character` field reporting the per-beneficiary breakdown across franked / cgt / ordinary. Five FY-end states pinned: no pools / pools no streaming / valid streaming (flag off) / invalid resolution / streaming without pools. 6 new tests. **324 tests passing** (318 → 324). POST endpoint validates + passes streaming body through. After 41e.4, the v1 caveat *"Tax allocated proportionally across entities; exact Div 6/6E trust distribution math lands with Phase 41e"* on the Money Flow Sankey is now resolvable — slice that wires Sankey to per-entity streaming dispatch is queued.

POST endpoint accepts `highIncomeSuper` body.

New `EntityTaxFacts.smsfContributions` field (concessional + non-concessional YTD + total super balance + carry-forward). SMSF entities with contribution data return real CapTrackingResult: cap headroom + s291-20(3) carry-forward (TSB < $500k threshold) + s292-85(2) bring-forward eligibility + excess-contribution tax warnings. Citations: ITAA 1997 s291-20 + s292-85 + SIS Pt 8. UC-SMSF-SOLE-PURPOSE flag replaces placeholder UC-ENTITY-SMSF — sole purpose test (s62) + in-house asset 5% cap + LRBA per PCG 2016/5 still UNCOMPUTED (lands in 41e.11). POST endpoint accepts `smsfContributions` body. 5 new router tests. **303 → 308 tests passing.**
- **Phase 41e.1 slice D-2 — wire CGT into `entityTaxRouter` SHIPPED (PR #650). Closes 41e.1.** New optional `EntityTaxFacts.cgtEvents` + `carryForwardCapitalLosses` + `smsfIsComplying` + `isForeignResident` fields. New optional `EntityTaxPosition.cgtResult` independent of `result` so a COMPANY entity (income tax UNCOMPUTED — that's 41e.7 territory) can carry a fully-computed CGT figure with **0% discount per s115-280**. For TRUST entities with BOTH `trustDistribution` AND `cgtEvents`, the router populates BOTH `result` (Div 6 distribution) AND `cgtResult` (Div 115 net capital gain) with deduplicated cumulative citations. POST `/api/tax/entity/[entityId]` extended to accept `cgtEvents` + `carryForwardCapitalLosses` body fields. **First demonstration of the audit's "never false silence" complement to "never false numbers"** — when an entity's income-tax dispatch is still UNCOMPUTED, the CGT side calc still surfaces with full citations + UNCOMPUTED-flag transparency. 8 new router tests pinning the new dispatch contract: PERSONAL_NAME with cgtEvents → 50% discount applied; COMPANY with cgtEvents → cgtResult populated + result still null (income tax UNCOMPUTED); SMSF complying → 33⅓%; TRUST with both inputs → both populated independently with deduplicated citations; losses > gains → carry-forward residual; prior-year losses applied via body field; no cgtEvents → cgtResult undefined; empty array → undefined. **303 tests total passing** (295 → 303). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` §10.13 slice D-2 row, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status flip ("**41e.1 COMPLETE**"), `07_API_STANDARDS.md` §15 (POST endpoint row updated to mention D-2 fields). **Phase 41e.2 (SMSF contribution caps) starts next.**
- **Phase 41e.1 slice D-1 — wire `trustDistribution` into `entityTaxRouter` SHIPPED (PR #649).** First user-testable Div 6 surface. Extends `EntityTaxFacts` with optional `trustDistribution` field (`trustNetIncome` + `beneficiaries[]` + `hasFamilyTrustElection?`). When provided for DISCRETIONARY_TRUST or UNIT_TRUST entities, the router runs `allocateTrustDistribution` (slice C) and produces a real `EntityTaxPosition.result` with per-beneficiary breakdown + s95/s97 citations + the always-on UC-S100A-RISK / UC-DIV-6E-STREAMING flags. Without distribution data, trust entities still flag UNCOMPUTED — backward-compatible with slice 41e.0 D's existing GET endpoint. New `entityHasConditionalComputedTax(type)` helper exposes the conditional capability matrix. New **`POST /api/tax/entity/[entityId]`** handler accepts a JSON body with `trustDistribution` so the wiring is testable via curl until a UI captures distribution data: `curl -X POST -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -d '{"trustDistribution":{"trustNetIncome":100000,"beneficiaries":[{"id":"b1","name":"Sarah","presentlyEntitledShare":1.0}],"hasFamilyTrustElection":true}}' /api/tax/entity/<trustId>` returns the computed Div 6 allocation with citations. 6 new router tests covering: TRUST without data → UNCOMPUTED, TRUST with data → computed, UNIT_TRUST with data → pro-rata, partial entitlement → s99A penalty, COMPANY/SMSF still UNCOMPUTED, PERSONAL_NAME ignores trustDistribution. **295 tests total passing** (289 → 295). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` §10.13 slice D-1 row, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status, `07_API_STANDARDS.md` §15 (new POST endpoint row) all updated in the same PR. Slice D-2 (CGT events into router) is the final 41e.1 slice.
- **Phase 41e.1 trust distribution slice C SHIPPED (PR #647).** New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/trustDistribution.ts` exporting `allocateTrustDistribution(input)` + `getDistributableAmount(result)` + `S99A_TRUSTEE_PENALTY_RATE` constant. Implements **ITAA 1936 s95** (net income definition), **s97** (presently-entitled beneficiary assessment at marginal rate), **s99A** (trustee penalty rate 47% on undistributed residual when no beneficiary is presently entitled). Validates beneficiary shares — throws on negative or sum > 1.0 (deed over-distribution) with floating-point tolerance to allow `1/3 + 1/3 + 1/3`. Always flags `UC-S100A-RISK` (full reimbursement-agreement zone classifier per TR 2022/4 + PCG 2022/2 lands in 41e.5; rationale wording differs for FTE vs non-FTE trusts) and `UC-DIV-6E-STREAMING` (character allocation lands in 41e.4 — v1 distributes franking + capital gains generically pro-rata to net-income share, not streamed to specific beneficiaries). Flags `UC-S98-TRUSTEE-ASSESSMENT` when any beneficiary is non-resident or under legal disability. 20 unit tests covering basic distribution (single + 2-way + 3-way), s99A penalty (zero entitlement / partial entitlement), validation errors, all UNCOMPUTED flag triggers, citation rules (s99A appears only when residual > 0), helper functions, edge cases (zero income, zero with beneficiaries). **289 tests total passing** (269 → 289). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment, all relevant docs updated in this same PR: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` §10.13 slice C row, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status callout. Slice D (router wiring — first user-visible 41e.1 surface) is next.
- **Phase 41e.1 capital loss netting slice B SHIPPED (PR #645).** New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/capitalLossNetting.ts` exporting `applyCapitalLossNetting(input)`. Implements **ITAA 1997 s100-50** (loss-method ordering: prior-year + current-year losses applied to gains; FIFO carry-forward residual), **ITAA 1997 s115-100** (discount applied to NET gain after losses, NOT to gross gains then summed — the consumer-tax mistake explicitly caught by a dedicated test), **ITAA 1997 Div 102-A** (assessable net capital gain). Composes slice A's `calculateCgtDiscount` per entity. When losses exceed gains, surfaces the unconsumed residual via `carryForwardOut` for the next FY's CGT register. Mixed holding periods raise `UC-CGT-MIXED-HOLDING` with proportional discount applied to the qualifying-share. 18 unit tests including the critical **s115-100 ordering pin**: gain $100k + loss $30k under 50% discount = $35k assessable, not $20k. **269 tests total passing** (251 → 269). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment, all relevant docs updated in this same PR: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` §10.13 slice B row, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status callout updated.
- **Phase 41e.1 Div 115 CGT discount slice A — entity-aware rate dispatch SHIPPED (PR #644).** First **rule** sub-PR after the 41e.0 foundation. Pure additive: new module `lib/tax-engine/divisions/cgtDiscount.ts` exporting `calculateCgtDiscount(input)` + `getCgtDiscountRate(entityType, isComplying?)`. Per-entity rates: PERSONAL_NAME / SOLE_TRADER / DISCRETIONARY_TRUST / UNIT_TRUST / PARTNERSHIP → **50%** (ITAA 1997 s115-25); SMSF complying → **33⅓%** (ITAA 1997 s115-100); SMSF non-complying → **0%** (s115-100 carve-out); COMPANY → **0%** (ITAA 1997 s115-280 explicitly cited in result so the boundary footer can show exactly why); any entity < 12 months held → 0% (s115-25 universal gate). Foreign-resident raises an `UC-FOREIGN-RESIDENT-CGT` UncomputedFlag pointing at Subdiv 115-D apportionment as a future sub-PR rather than computing the proportional rule (the calc still returns the standard discount; UNCOMPUTED is surfaced for the user). 24 unit tests in `tests/tax-engine/divisions/cgtDiscount.test.ts` covering every entity branch + the holding-period boundary (exactly 12 months → discount applies; 11 months → no discount) + SMSF complying/non-complying split + foreign-resident UNCOMPUTED + edge cases (zero gain, negative gain trusted to caller). No consumers wired yet — slice D of 41e.1 will replace the COMPANY / DISCRETIONARY_TRUST UNCOMPUTED branches in `entityTaxRouter` to call this for CGT-event inputs. **251 tests total passing** (227 → 251, +24). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment from PR #637, all relevant docs updated in the same PR: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` new §10.13 capturing the slice + the per-entity rate table + queue of remaining slices, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status flip (41e.1 slice A in flight, slices B/C/D queued), this entry.

(2) `lib/tax-engine/boundaries/index.ts` — canonical AFSL/TPB/NCCP boundary footer assembler. `BOUNDARY_STATEMENT` constant centralises legal copy ("These figures are general information only — not personal financial, tax, or credit advice. Every `*Input` interface gained an `ownerEntityId?: string | null` field — additive, optional, nullable. Existing callers that don't populate the field see `undefined`, omit the new filter param, and get identical pre-41e behaviour. New callers (the upcoming `entityTaxRouter` in slice D, the per-rule modules in 41e.1+) populate the field from the Prisma read and pass the filter. 18 new tests in `tests/calculations/aggregatorEntityScoping.test.ts` pin both halves of the contract: (1) omitting the filter reproduces household-wide totals exactly, (2) providing it returns only matching items, (3) **`e1.total + e2.total === household.total`** — the structural correctness guarantee that proves no double-counting. tsc clean. **201 tests passing** (was 183, +18). New exported `validateParentChain(entityId, proposedParentId, client)` walker implementing 4 rules (self-parent forbidden, no cycle in chain, max depth 10, advisory type-compatibility deferred to wizard layer). Returns structured `SELF_PARENT` / `CYCLE_DETECTED` / `MAX_DEPTH_EXCEEDED` / `PARENT_NOT_FOUND` errors. Wired into both `createEntity()` and `updateEntity()` inside the same Prisma client/transaction (audit §7.3 — prevents TOCTOU races); subsumes the old self-parent check. Defence-in-depth DB CHECK constraint via additive migration `20260506110000_legal_entity_no_self_parent` — `CHECK (id <> parentEntityId OR parentEntityId IS NULL)` — catches the simplest cycle at storage layer regardless of write path. §12.11 N/A — pure additive guard. 11 tests in `tests/legalEntityService/parentChain.test.ts` (the 8 mandated by audit §7.5 plus 3 extras for completeness — null parent / PARENT_NOT_FOUND surfaces / pre-existing cyclic chain detected without infinite-loop). Uses in-memory `fakeClient` stubbing `legalEntity.findUnique` against a chain map; pure unit, no DB. 183 tests total now passing. tsc clean.
- **Phase 41e.0 foundation slice A — permissions + entity-aware orchestration types SHIPPED (PR #634).** First slice of 41e.0. Pure additive type contract; zero consumer changes; zero behaviour changes; sets up the contract for slices B/C/D + 41e.1 → 41e.17. Two new permissions in `lib/auth/permissions.ts` — `tax_data.read` (mirrors `report.read` audience for VIEWER+) gating per-entity tax position queries, `tax_data.write` (CONTRIBUTOR+) gating computational mutations like trust-distribution composition or CGT-disposal calc. JSDoc clarifies these gate route access; CDR sanitisation rules of CLAUDE.md §13.3 still govern CDR-content visibility. Six new exported interfaces in `lib/tax-engine/types.ts` per architecture doc §4 + audit doc §6.2: `AuthorityCitation` (primary AU authority — ITAA / SIS / TR / TD / PCG / PS LA / state acts — attached to every rule result, consumed by AFSL/TPB/NCCP boundaries renderer in slice D); `FYReference` (FY-indexed lookup contract, no hard-coded thresholds); `EntityTaxFacts` (per-entity dispatcher input with optional fields populating progressively as 41e.1+ ship rule modules — CGT events, trust distribution resolutions, Div 7A loans, LRBA arrangements); `EntityTaxPosition` (output of single-entity dispatch, wraps Phase 20 `TaxPositionResult` for PERSONAL_NAME, net-new shapes for COMPANY/TRUST/SMSF land per sub-PR); `UncomputedFlag` (audit-friendly "deliberately not computed" structure per audit §10.3 — UI surfaces as plain-English badges, never false numbers); `MasterTaxPosition` (household-wide roll-up; canonical replacement for `buildTaxSummary()` once 41e.17 ships the orchestrator). Two intentional deviations from architecture doc pseudocode: `EntityTaxPosition.result` typed `unknown` (sub-PRs refine without churn); `EntityTaxFacts.incomes/expenses/depreciations` inlined as structural rows rather than `import`-from-position to avoid circular imports. Both documented inline.

- **Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR C — buildTaxSummary delegation SHIPPED (PR #630 merged).** Resolves audit C-1 — the headline regression trap. `buildTaxSummary()` no longer reimplements brackets inline at `masterFinancialService.ts:1012-1077`; it now delegates to `calculateTaxPosition()` from the canonical Phase 20 tax engine and adapts the result back into the legacy `TaxSummary` shape. Behaviour change is intentional + documented per audit §9.2 capture-before-refactor protocol: the new code includes Medicare Levy (Health Insurance Levy Act 1982) + LITO/SAPTO/franking/foreign offsets (ITAA 1997 Div 126-H/126-L/207/770) + franking gross-up + categorised deductions, none of which the old inline code applied. Numbers now match `/api/tax/position` for the same user. Consumer shape preserved — `TaxSummary` interface unchanged; every consumer (dashboard, AI advisor, Sankey) continues working without modification. Adapter pattern is intentional and stays until 41e.0+ swaps consumers directly onto `TaxPositionResult`.
- **Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR B — consumer migration SHIPPED (PR #629 merged).** Resolves 6 audit findings in one slice. Migrated every consumer to `getCurrentTaxYearConfig()` / `getMarginalRate()` instead of hard-coded values: `/api/tax/super/optimize/route.ts` (6× `0.15` + `60400` co-contrib), `/api/tax/super/contributions/route.ts` (`0.85` keyed off contribution's actual FY), `lib/cfo/decisionSupport/taxIntegration.ts` (concessional cap, Div 293, super tax rate), `lib/strategy/analyzers/taxAnalyzer.ts` (CGT discount + marginal rate now derived from user's actual income via `cashflowSummary.totalAnnualIncome`), `app/dashboard/tax/page.tsx` (brackets table dynamic; SG / caps / FY label / Medicare rate all config-driven; salary-sacrifice opportunity card subtracts canonical `superContributionsTaxRate`). **Resolves: C-2 (concessional cap divergence), H-1 (×7 hard-coded `0.15`), H-2 (co-contrib threshold ×2), H-3 (11.5% SG rate), H-4 (hard-coded brackets table), H-5 (assumed 30% marginal rate). H-6 ($3k/property depreciation) renamed to `HEURISTIC_PROPERTY_DEPRECIATION_PER_YEAR` and flagged as `UC-PROPERTY-DEPRECIATION` in audit §10.3.** Behaviour-preserving: every replaced hard-code returns the same numeric value via the new config path on FY24-25 (slice A populated FY24-25 with values matching what consumers were hard-coding). Grep audit confirms no remaining `* 0.85`, `* 0.15`, `27500`, `60400`, `250000` literals.

- **Phase 32C PR4b — Ask-a-Professional button + picker SHIPPED.** Closes Up Next #14. Connects the consumer-side AI Guide to the professional surface — the loop the marketplace needed to feel real.
