Org-attached users (active+granted OrganizationClient row) see ONLY their org's roster — leaky-funnel guardrail enforced server-side per Up Next #15 strategic decision. D2C users see top-3 best-fit APPROVED marketplace listings biased by the calling `context` label (open-ended map: tax / retirement / refinance / property / smsf / wealth / trust / business / estate / insurance / home-loan / investment-loan / general). New API route `app/api/ask-a-pro/candidates` — `withPermission('report.read')`. New primitive `<AskAProfessionalButton context={...} variant="primary"|"compact"|"icon" />` (three variants for primary CTA / inline secondary / icon-only) + `<AskAProfessionalDialog />` picker (right-edge ≥sm / bottom-sheet <sm with sticky header, body-scroll lock, Esc to close, backdrop click to close, prefers-reduced-motion-aware via Tailwind `motion-safe:*` utilities). Picker branches on scope: org-scope shows assigned advisor highlighted in emerald glass tile + the rest of the active roster (excludes VIEWER seats); public-scope shows 3 best-fit cards with rating + tagline + discipline label, "See all professionals →" footer link to `/marketplace`. Click on a member → `/portal-message?memberId=<id>` placeholder (in-app conversation surface ships in PR4d); click on a listing → `/marketplace/[slug]` (existing Connect CTA is the entry to the full request lifecycle in PR4c). Wired into `components/cfo/AdviceRecommendationCard.tsx` as a compact-variant button next to "Ask a follow-up", with a `CATEGORY_TO_ASK_A_PRO_CONTEXT` map (tax → tax, debt → refinance, property → property, investment → wealth, risk → insurance, etc.) so each AI advice category opens the picker pre-biased to the right specialisation. Unauthenticated D2C path returns 401 from the API; dialog falls through to a "Create a free Monitrax account" nudge linking to `/register`. Pitch playbook Step 6 demo path now reads end-to-end. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (exit 0); `npx next build` green; `/api/ask-a-pro/candidates` registered. **Phase 32C PR4c (request lifecycle + lead-fee billing) unblocked.** ZERO new dependencies — reused existing patterns; the dialog uses Tailwind `motion-safe:*` utilities instead of pulling framer-motion just for animations.
- **Phase 32C PR4a — Professional Marketplace SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes Up Next #13. First Phase 32C deliverable on the Demo-Complete Critical Path, ships in parallel with Session D's Phase 41 build (zero territory overlap — marketplace owns `ProfessionalListing` + `ProfessionalRating`, entity layer owns `LegalEntity`). Schema: two new Prisma models + four supporting enums (`ListingStatus`, `ProfessionalSpecialisation` × 15 values, `ListingTargetTier` × 4 values, `ListingRegion` × 12 values) + five new AuditAction enum values for the lifecycle (UPDATED / SUBMITTED / APPROVED / REJECTED / SUSPENDED). Matching additive migration `prisma/migrations/20260505140000_add_professional_marketplace/migration.sql` — no destructive writes, no row touches, §12.11 checklist N/A. Service `lib/services/marketplaceService.ts` (~440 lines) consolidates Org-side / admin-side / public scopes into one canonical service; one engine, scope is a parameter, not a fork. Submit-time validation: ≥10-char tagline, ≥100-char blurb, ≥1 specialisation + region, discipline-conditional compliance number (AFSL for FINANCIAL_ADVISOR, credit rep for MORTGAGE_BROKER, TPB for TAX_AGENT/ACCOUNTING_FIRM); editing APPROVED → flips back to PENDING_REVIEW (admin re-checks before re-publish); editing REJECTED → returns to DRAFT. Six new API routes: portal `GET/PUT /api/portal/organizations/[orgId]/marketplace-listing` + `POST .../submit`; admin `GET /api/admin/marketplace/listings` + `GET/POST /api/admin/marketplace/listings/[id]` (POST takes `{ action: 'approve' | 'reject' | 'suspend' }`); public `GET /api/marketplace/listings` + `GET /api/marketplace/listings/[slug]`. Permissions: portal `marketplace:listing:read|write|submit` (read = all roles incl. VIEWER; write = OWNER+ADMIN; submit = OWNER only — anti-poaching guardrail mirroring `team:invite`); admin `marketplace:listings:read|approve|reject|suspend` (SUPER_ADMIN full; SUPPORT_ADMIN + VIEWER read-only). Three new UI surfaces — (1) `/portal/marketplace/listing` Org-side editor with `MarketplaceListingEditor` client component (Apple-glass aesthetic matching `PracticeGlassCard`, status-aware form locking when PENDING_REVIEW, discipline-conditional compliance fields, accessible checkbox groups for facets, sticky bottom action bar, REJECTED/SUSPENDED feedback panels with admin reasoning shown inline, auto-derived publicSlug until user explicitly types one); (2) `/admin/marketplace/listings` admin queue (defaults to PENDING_REVIEW filter, status + discipline dropdowns, full-text search, click-to-drill into `/admin/marketplace/listings/[id]` detail page with one-tap deeplinks to ASIC moneysmart register / TPB public register / ABR for the AFSL / credit rep / TPB / ABN respectively, manual cross-check checkboxes that record `asicCrossCheckedAt` + `tpbCrossCheckedAt` for the auditor trail, free-text `verificationNotes` scratchpad, approve/reject/suspend action buttons with required reason text); (3) `/marketplace` public browse (D2C discovery surface with hero copy, search + discipline + region + specialisation filters, listing cards with rating + accepted-request count + specialisation chips + region preview, sorted by averageRating desc + acceptedRequestsCount desc + alpha) and `/marketplace/[slug]` public detail (full blurb, specialisations as chips, target-tier "best fit for" list, regions, recent ratings, "Connect" CTA pointing at `/signup?intent=connect&listing=<slug>` — Phase 32C PR4b's `<AskAProfessionalButton>` plugs in here). Public surfaces have a compliance footer explaining the ASIC + TPB cross-check + AFSL/credit/TPB licence boundary on every page. **Architecture decision: zero new dependencies.** Recharts (already in deps from Phase 41d) is reused only via existing imports; no new packages added. Listings are invite-only at v1 — Monitrax-curated 10-20 launch professionals; the admin approve/reject flow is the gate. **Architecture decision: lead-fee tiers stored per-listing.** Per-Org overrides at admin-approval time (rare; defaults stand for most listings) — surface is in the admin detail page if needed. Pitch playbook Step 6 placeholder populated. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (exit 0). `npx next build` green with all 11 marketplace routes registered (`/admin/marketplace/listings`, `/admin/marketplace/listings/[id]`, `/api/admin/marketplace/listings`, `/api/admin/marketplace/listings/[id]`, `/api/marketplace/listings`, `/api/marketplace/listings/[slug]`, `/api/portal/organizations/[orgId]/marketplace-listing`, `/api/portal/organizations/[orgId]/marketplace-listing/submit`, `/marketplace`, `/marketplace/[slug]`, `/portal/marketplace/listing`). **DEFERRED to PROD-ready:** automated ASIC/TPB API cross-check (manual at v1), Cloud Storage for hero images (placeholder field stored), email notifications on approve/reject (admin reaches out manually at v1). - **Phase 41g — Adviser drill-in entity layer SHIPPED (this PR).** `/portal/clients/[id]/view` extended with a 3-tab toggle: **Structure** (default — the Phase 41c `EntityTree` populated with the CLIENT's data) | **Money Flow** (the Phase 41d `MoneyFlowSankey`) | **Dashboard** (the existing `ClientCanonicalDashboard` from 32B PR3). The adviser sees the entity tree FIRST when opening a client view — per the brief, *"the adviser cannot give wealth advice without seeing the structure first; this surfaces it prominently."* Two new portal endpoints — `GET /api/portal/clients/[id]/entities` (returns `{ entities, members }` for the client) and `GET /api/portal/clients/[id]/money-flow` (returns the per-entity flow shape) — both delegate to the same canonical services (`listEntitiesForUser`, `getMoneyFlow`) but pass the client's userId, not the caller's. New shared helper `lib/portal/adviserClientAccess.ts` extracts the consent + membership + role + assignment guard from the existing `/snapshot` route so all three portal endpoints share one canonical access check; the snapshot route was refactored to use it (reviewers reject any new portal client-data endpoint that doesn't route through `verifyAdviserClientAccess`). Audit: piggybacks on the page-level `PRO_DASHBOARD_VIEW` row already written by the snapshot endpoint — no per-component log spam (if component-level access logs are ever required for compliance, add new action codes like `PRO_ENTITY_VIEW` and emit them at the route layer). **Read-only in adviser view** — advisers cannot edit a client's entity layer (no AddCTA, no EditDialog wiring; tile clicks are no-ops). Future Phase 41 slice may surface a "Suggest a structural change" affordance that opens an Ask-a-Pro thread, but that's a separate workstream. Drill-in page now fetches snapshot + entities + flow in parallel; entities/flow failures don't block the Dashboard tab (the tree handles zero entities, Sankey handles `isEmpty`). Pitch playbook Step 2/3 updated to reflect that the entity tree is reachable from the same drill-in URL — no tab switching, no separate page. **Phase 41h (AI entity-aware diagnosis) and 41f (Xero/MYOB integration) are now the only Phase 41 surfaces left for demo-complete pre-pitch.** tsc clean.
- **Phase 41d — Money Flow Sankey at `/dashboard/entities` SHIPPED (PR #617 merged 2026-05-05).** Demo-Complete Critical Path Wk-6/7 deliverable + the **second wow moment** in the lighthouse pitch (Step 4). 3-stage visualisation: **Income sources** (Salary / Rental / Investment / Other) → **Legal entities** (role-coloured, matching the Phase 41c tree palette so the two surfaces share visual language) → **Outflows** (Tax / Essential expenses / Discretionary / Loan repayments / Surplus). New service `lib/services/moneyFlowService.ts` exporting `getMoneyFlow(userId)` — pulls Income / Expense / Loan rows in parallel, classifies income by source label (SALARY → Salary; RENTAL/RENT → Rental; INVESTMENT → Investment; everything else → Other), aggregates expenses by entity × essential/discretionary, computes loan repayments per entity from `minRepayment` annualised, allocates tax (PAYG withholding) proportionally to each entity's share of taxable income, and computes surplus as the residual. SSOT: uses canonical `toAnnual` from `lib/utils/frequencies.ts`; reads raw rows directly so per-entity attribution stays accurate (the master snapshot's `incomeBreakdown` aggregates across entities). New API route `app/api/money-flow/route.ts` — thin wrapper, `withPermission('report.read')` (same gate as `/api/master-snapshot`). New presentational component `components/entities/MoneyFlowSankey.tsx` rendered with **`recharts <Sankey>`** (already in deps — `@nivo/sankey` and `d3-sankey` evaluated and rejected per CLAUDE.md §12.7+§12.8: zero new dependencies). Custom Node renderer outputs role-coloured Apple-glass tiles (PERSONAL warm amber → OPERATING emerald → HOLDING indigo → SUPERANNUATION violet → INVESTMENT fuchsia, matching the 41c tree palette) with AUD labels outside the column. Custom Tooltip shows `Source → Target $X per year`. Headline-summary chip strip above the canvas (Income / Tax / Essentials / Discretionary / Loans / Surplus or Deficit) so the viewer reads the totals before the flows. **v1 tax allocation is proportional, not Div 6/6E-correct** — flagged inline as an italic caveat below the canvas: *"Annual reference period. Tax allocated proportionally across entities; exact Div 6/6E trust distribution math lands with Phase 41e."* Honest, audit-friendly. Page-level changes: new tab toggle on `/dashboard/entities` (Structure | Money Flow); tab state lifted to the page; lazy-fetch when the tab is activated; cache invalidates on entity mutation (add/edit/remove from Structure tab). Same Bearer-token auth pattern as the entities API call. Empty state: friendly "Not enough data to draw your money flow yet" hero when the user has no income or no expenses. `prefers-reduced-motion` honoured (entrance fade collapses). Phase 41e (full entity-aware tax engine — replaces the proportional v1 allocation with Div 6/6E + s100A + Div 7A correctness) and 41h (AI entity-aware diagnosis composing the same flow shape) unblocked. tsc clean.

### 2026-05-04
- **Phase 41c — Interactive Entity Tree at `/dashboard/entities` SHIPPED (this PR).** Reza directive 2026-05-04: *"Yes it should replace, the tree view should be an interactive and live tree view that user can view, track and navigate through the tree view."* Replaces the 41b list view with a 3-row glass-tile tree (People → Legal Entities → owned-objects chips inline), SVG Bézier connectors between rows, dashed fuchsia paths for trustee→trust corporate hierarchy via the `parentEntityId` self-FK. New `components/entities/EntityTree.tsx` (~520 lines) and shared `components/entities/types.ts` (Entity / HouseholdMember / OwnedObjectsCount / `ROLE_PALETTE` per-role colour table). Page-level changes: parallel fetch of `/api/household-members` so the People row reflects real household composition (with a "You" anchor fallback when no members exist); list-rendering block (98 lines) replaced with a single `<EntityTree>` call (5 lines); entity-tile remove buttons retired in favour of a Remove affordance in the `EntityFormDialog` footer (canonical entry point now: tile click → edit dialog → either Save or Remove). Role palette: PERSONAL warm amber / OPERATING emerald / HOLDING indigo / SUPERANNUATION violet / INVESTMENT fuchsia — colour-blind-readable with text labels. Owned-objects rendered as in-tile chips (clickable, drill to `/dashboard/properties` / `/dashboard/balances` / `/dashboard/investments/accounts` / `/dashboard/assets`). Mobile fallback (<md): suppresses SVG, stacks tiles vertically, surfaces "trustee: …" labels for corporate hierarchy. `prefers-reduced-motion` honoured throughout (motion lines render but don't animate). Built with framer-motion (already in deps); zero new dependencies — `reactflow` / `@xyflow/react` evaluated and rejected per CLAUDE.md §12.7+§12.8 (custom code is simpler than a 150kb dep for a 3-row tree). v1 People→Entity edge heuristic: PERSONAL_NAME entities match by case-insensitive name to a HouseholdMember; non-PERSONAL_NAME entities fan out from every PERSONAL_NAME entity's matched person (the "principals"). Phase 41e will replace this with proper shareholder/beneficiary fields once those land in the schema. Page header chip flipped from `Sparkles` to `TreePine` to match the new metaphor. Pitch playbook Step 3 verbiage updated with the real tree visual. Phase 41d (Money Flow Sankey) unblocked. tsc clean.
- **Phase 33c (light) — Per-article Save-as-PDF SHIPPED (PR #613).** Closes Up Next #21 (light scope; ZIP bundle deferred to PROD). Demo-Complete Critical Path week-13 deliverable — completes the in-app help triplet (33a Help Center + 33b drawer + 33c PDF) so every help article in the demo env is auditor-downloadable. Three new files: `app/print/help/[...slug]/page.tsx` (sibling top-level print route — bypasses `app/help/layout.tsx` chrome so the print body is genuinely clean, no CSS hide-the-nav hacks; uses the SAME `lib/help/markdown.ts` zero-dep renderer the screen view uses; A4-targeted print CSS with `@page 18mm/16mm/22mm/16mm` margins, page-break-inside: avoid on `<pre>` / blockquote / list items, page-break-after: avoid on headings, emerald link colour preserved in print; document header carries Monitrax brand + audience + reviewed date; document footer carries canonical URL provenance which is what auditors expect from a compliance pack), `app/print/help/[...slug]/layout.tsx` (tiny white-bg wrapper with `robots: { index: false, follow: false }` metadata so search engines don't see the print pages), `app/print/help/[...slug]/AutoPrint.tsx` (small client component — waits for `document.fonts.ready` then triggers `window.print()` on next animation frame so the user lands on the page with the Save-as-PDF dialog already open; re-triggerable via the toolbar "Open print dialog" link so a dismissed dialog isn't a dead-end; 80ms paint-buffer delay so the toolbar paints before the modal). One file edit: `app/help/[...slug]/page.tsx` gains a "Download as PDF" link in the article header (`print:hidden` so it doesn't leak into print, target="_blank" + rel="noopener", inline 16×16 download chevron SVG, opens `/print/help/<audience>/<articleSlug>`). Static-generated for every article in `generateStaticParams()` — 8 paths in this build (3 consumer + 1 org-admin + 1 org-professional + 5 compliance). `npx next build` green; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. **Architecture decision: zero new dependencies.** Evaluated `@react-pdf/renderer` — 1,005 transitive packages with 36 vulnerabilities including 2 critical / 9 high. Rejected per CLAUDE.md §12.7 (managed-service / minimum-custom-code) — the browser-native print-to-PDF pipeline is what Stripe Docs / MDN / GitHub use for the same reason: deterministic, accessible, respects user paper-size + orientation preferences, and the resulting PDF stays selectable + searchable. Compliance auditors care about evidence + provenance, not pixel polish. **Architecture decision: print route OUTSIDE `/help/*`.** Initial attempt placed it at `app/help/[...slug]/print/page.tsx`; Next.js rejected this with "Catch-all must be the last part of the URL." Pivoted to `/print/help/<audience>/<slug>` — sibling top-level route — which is structurally cleaner anyway: the print view is a genuinely different surface (clean document, no chrome) and a top-level `/print/...` prefix is a natural home for future Phase 33f DOCX templates. **Pitch playbook Step 8** — compliance pack table updated to mention the per-article download capability so the lighthouse adviser sees it during the meeting. Reza directive: parallel work to Session D's Phase 41 build — zero territory overlap (Phase 41 owns entity layer, Phase 33c owns help system).
- **Phase 41b — Onboarding wizard "How is your wealth held?" + standalone entity management SHIPPED (PR #612).** Wk-3 deliverable on the Demo-Complete Critical Path. Three new surfaces + three new API routes + one new service slice + one new validators module, all tied together by the Phase 41a foundation. (1) **Wizard step** at `components/onboarding/wizard/steps/EntitiesStep.tsx`, slotted between Household and Properties so subsequent steps can attach to entities. Captures type (LegalEntityType), role (LegalEntityRole), warm display name, optional ABN (with mod-89 checksum validation), optional ACN (with ASIC complement checksum), optional opt-in TFN (encrypted via `lib/security/tfnEncryption.ts` per CLAUDE.md §13 — never logged, never sent to AI, never displayed in plaintext after entry), trustee→trust parent link via wizard-local `parentEntityTempId`. Smart default: empty entity list lets bulk-create fall through to `getDefaultLegalEntityId()` and attach everything to the user's PERSONAL_NAME entity (idempotent with the 41a backfill). Behaviour-psychology copy throughout — "most Australian wealth-builders hold their assets across more than one legal entity" (normalising) not "you should have set up a structure" (shaming). (2) **Two-pass writer** in `app/api/onboarding/bulk-create/route.ts` — pass 1 inserts all entities without parent FK, pass 2 maps wizard temp-ids to real DB ids and wires up trustee→trust corporate links. New `wizardEntityMap` in scope for future wizard steps to attach owned rows to non-default entities. (3) **Standalone surface** at `app/dashboard/entities/page.tsx` — "My Structure" page added to TRACK-stage sidebar as a child of My Accounts per CLAUDE.md §14.2 (entities are foundational to "what you own"). Cards list every entity with type/role/ABN/ACN/parent + owned-objects summary; add/edit dialog mirrors the wizard step's UX; remove flow uses AlertDialog with the destructive-remove guard's friendly counts surfaced inline ("This entity owns 3 properties, 2 loans..."). (4) **API routes** — new `app/api/entities/route.ts` (GET list / POST create) and `app/api/entities/[id]/route.ts` (GET / PUT / DELETE). All behind canonical `withPermission('entity.read|write|delete')` (new perms added to `lib/auth/permissions.ts` mirroring property/loan rows). All state-changing routes write `logCRUD` audit rows with `sanitizeCdrMetadata`-wrapped payloads — TFN value NEVER appears in metadata, only `hasTfn: boolean`. DELETE returns 409 Conflict with structured `counts` object when the entity has owned objects (instead of a raw P2003 FK violation), plus a hard "can't remove last PERSONAL_NAME entity" guard so the default-owner fallback always exists. PUT/DELETE use `withPermission<RouteContext>('perm', async (request, auth, context) => { const { id } = await context!.params; })` per the loans route pattern. (5) **Service extension** at `lib/services/legalEntityService.ts` — adds `listEntitiesForUser` (returns `LegalEntitySummary[]` with `_count` aggregation across all 7 owned-row tables; never returns `tfnEncrypted` value, only `hasTfn: boolean`), `createEntity` / `updateEntity` (encryptTfn-wrapped, parent-ownership-validated), `deleteEntity` (throws `EntityHasOwnedObjectsError` with the friendly counts before the FK guard would fire). Re-exports added to `lib/services/index.ts`. (6) **AU validators** — new canonical module `lib/utils/auValidators.ts`: `isValidAbn` (11-digit modulus-89 per ATO ABN spec), `isValidAcn` (9-digit ASIC complement checksum per ASIC RG 22), `isValidTfnFormat` (8/9-digit format check; checksum is not publicly available per ATO security-by-obscurity policy), plus `formatAbn` (`XX XXX XXX XXX` ATO display) and `formatAcn` (`XXX XXX XXX` ASIC display). Used by both client-side wizard validation and server-side route validation — single SSOT. (7) **AIHelper** entities-step content added so the wizard's contextual help button works on the new step. tsc clean. Phase 41c (Entity Tree visualisation) unblocked — next session decision: replace list at `/dashboard/entities` with the tree view, or keep both surfaces (list = management, tree at sub-route = visualisation). Pitch playbook Step 3 entity-seed sub-bullets populated for all 3 archetypes (Sarah Kim Pty Ltd; Mei Family Trust + corporate trustee + SMSF; Olivia's full multi-entity stack with Discretionary + Unit + Pty Ltd + SMSF).

Two layout edits: `DashboardLayout.tsx` mounts the button alongside `AiChatButton` with audiences `[consumer, compliance]` (suppressed during onboarding wizard / welcome modal so it doesn't compete with primary onboarding affordances); `PortalLayoutClient.tsx` mounts with audiences `[org-admin, org-professional, org-client, consumer, compliance]`. - **Phase 41 Regulatory Architecture blueprint SHIPPED (doc-only PR).** New `docs/blueprint/PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` fixes the authority-mapped architecture for Phase 41e (entity-aware tax engine) + 41h (AI entity-aware diagnosis) before any regulatory code lands. Reza brief 2026-05-04 (post-Phase-41a): *"Make sure you have or going to fully incorporate the australian regulated laws for Phase 41 section. these laws and regulations should also be considering the other financial portfolio of the user and the relationships ... Monitrax don't just guess it will work based on the real laws and regulations."* Doc covers: §1 operating principles (authority over assumption, SSOT per rule, citation in code, ATO worked-example fixtures, AFSL/TPB/NCCP boundary structural-not-editorial, UNCOMPUTED register, FY-indexed thresholds); §2 regulatory surface mapped (ITAA 1936/1997 — Div 6, 6E, 7A, 115, 152, 296, Part IVA, s100A per TR 2022/4 + PCG 2022/2, trust + company loss rules, PSI per TR 2022/3, FTE/IEE; SIS Act — sole purpose, in-house asset 5% cap, LRBA per PCG 2016/5, contribution caps, TBC, Div 293, Div 296, preservation; per-state land tax + stamp duty + foreign surcharge; GST/BAS); §3 canonical module structure under `lib/calculations/tax/` (divisions/, super/, losses/, psi/, landTax/, stampDuty/, gst/, boundaries/, fixtures/); §4 canonical types (FYReference, EntityTaxFacts, TaxRuleResult, AuthorityCitation, MasterTaxPosition with `authoritySources` audit trail); §5 boundary enforcement (Gemini tool registry has NO recommendation tool; profession-aware AFSL/credit/TPB footer rendered everywhere personal-advice-shaped data appears); §6 cross-entity rules (trustee→trust corporate hierarchy, trust→beneficiary Div 6/6E/s100A composition, Pty Ltd→shareholder Div 7A decision tree, SMSF triumvirate sole-purpose+in-house-asset+LRBA); §7 FY-indexed threshold table (no hard-coded constants, single PR adds new FY entry annually); §8 fixture provenance (every fixture lifted verbatim from named ATO source — TD/TR/PCG/PS LA — with `extractedAt` + `lastReviewed` staleness flags); §9 versioning protocol (monthly Cloud Scheduler job flags citations >12mo old); §10 UNCOMPUTED register (initial entries: FTE ordering, Div 7A sub-trust UPE, multi-state land tax aggregation, foreign-resident beneficiary withholding, deceased estates, CGT event K6, GST margin scheme, stamp duty resettlement quantification); §11 implementation sequence (41e split into 16 sub-PRs: 5 demo cut at ~12 days + 11 PROD at ~28 days); §12 sign-off + maintenance (quarterly walk-through; reviewers reject 41e PRs that introduce rules not represented here). Up Next #29 (Phase 41e) updated to add this doc as a hard prerequisite + sub-PR sequence. No code in this PR — all subsequent 41e/h code references this doc in its file-header JSDoc.

One canonical engine, viewerContext is a parameter, not a fork. (2) Per-view audit writes BOTH `AuditLog` (`PRO_DASHBOARD_VIEW`, additive enum migration `20260504160000_add_pro_dashboard_view_action`) and `ClientAccessLog` so the 3-layer consent model (CDR / professional / per-view) stays intact per `docs/architecture/03_DATA_MODEL.md` §9.2. (3) New `/api/portal/clients/[id]/snapshot` route returns scoped snapshot + client + notes + tasks + lastReviewedAt + org profession in one round-trip; verifies caller seat owns the org link + role + assignment. (4) New page `app/portal/clients/[id]/view/page.tsx` renders the canonical consumer-dashboard primitives via new `ClientCanonicalDashboard` component (KPI strip + Health + Cashflow + Properties + Loans + Investments + Tax + Emergency Fund) with `AdviserOverlay` docked-right ≥md / bottom-sheet <md (scope summary + last-review timestamp + notes + tasks + profession-aware AFSL/credit/TPB compliance footer). Tiles outside the granted scope render as locked placeholders rather than disappearing — surfaces the next consent extension as the obvious next-best-action (CLAUDE.md §0 behaviour-psychology lens). (5) `components/portal/clients/ClientDetail.tsx` deleted (zero callers; closes Dead Code item). (6) Plan-tier gating in `lib/auth/guards.ts` via new `withPortalFeatureGate(feature, handler)` — reads `OrganizationPortalSettings.plan`, maps to canonical `PlanTier` (`STUDIO / PRACTICE / ENTERPRISE`) via new `lib/portal/planTier.ts` registry, returns 402 with `PLAN_TIER_REQUIRED` when the caller's tier doesn't unlock the feature. Tier registry: STUDIO (no SSO / no white-label / no API keys / 90-day audit window), PRACTICE (white-label + API keys + 365-day window), ENTERPRISE (everything + custom domain + 7yr window per CLAUDE.md §13.5). - **Phase 41a — `LegalEntity` schema + ownership backfill SHIPPED (this PR).** Foundation for the entity layer that everything in Phase 41b–h depends on. New Prisma model `LegalEntity` (uuid pk, userId FK cascade, name, type [PERSONAL_NAME / COMPANY / DISCRETIONARY_TRUST / UNIT_TRUST / SMSF / PARTNERSHIP / SOLE_TRADER], role [PERSONAL / HOLDING / OPERATING / INVESTMENT / SUPERANNUATION], abn?, acn?, tfnEncrypted?, tradingName?, establishedDate?, self-FK parentEntityId? for trustee→trust hierarchies, indexes on userId + type + parentEntityId). Two new enums `LegalEntityType` + `LegalEntityRole`. Added `ownerEntityId` (NOT NULL after backfill, FK with `ON DELETE RESTRICT`) to seven owned models — `Property`, `Loan`, `Account`, `InvestmentAccount`, `Asset`, `Income`, `Expense`. Matching migration `prisma/migrations/20260504130000_add_legal_entity/migration.sql` runs additively in five steps: (1) CREATE TYPE for both enums; (2) CREATE TABLE legal_entities + self-FK + user-FK + 3 indexes; (3) ADD COLUMN ownerEntityId NULLABLE on each owned table; (4) backfill — INSERT one PERSONAL_NAME LegalEntity per existing user via `gen_random_uuid()` named after `users.name`, then UPDATE every owned row WHERE `ownerEntityId IS NULL` to point at its user's PERSONAL_NAME entity (§12.11 IS-NULL guard ensures we only touch rows the migration just added the column to); (5) ALTER each ownerEntityId to NOT NULL + index + FK. New canonical service `lib/services/legalEntityService.ts` exporting `getDefaultLegalEntityId(userId, [tx])` — resolves the user's PERSONAL_NAME entity (creating one on demand for brand-new registrations between 41a deploy and 41b ship); accepts an optional transaction client so callers inside `prisma.$transaction` can compose. Eighteen call sites updated to provide `ownerEntityId` on every owned-row create: `app/api/properties|loans|accounts|assets|income|expenses|expenses/bulk|investments/accounts|onboarding/bulk-create|accounts/[id]/import|bank/import|basiq/sync|documents/analyze/confirm|recurring-payments/[id]/link|transactions/[id]/link/route.ts`, `lib/bank/recurringExpenseDetection.ts`, `lib/testing/loader.ts`, `prisma/seed-validation.ts`. TFN at-rest helper `lib/security/tfnEncryption.ts` mirrors the `MFAMethod.secret` Phase 10 pattern — `encryptTfn` (8/9-digit validation + base64 wrap), `decryptTfn` (digit-shape revalidation), `maskTfn` (display safe `***-***-XYZ`); single swap-point for KMS-backed CMEK upgrade when Up Next #3 lands. CLAUDE.md §13 hard rules enforced in code: `tfnEncrypted` is optional + default-off, never logged, never in audit metadata, never sent to AI. Doc sync complete: `docs/architecture/03_DATA_MODEL.md` extended with new §10 (Entity Layer — why an entity layer, the LegalEntity shape, the ownerEntityId pattern, migration & backfill, default-entity service, TFN handling, what 41b–h enables); `docs/pitch/LIGHTHOUSE_ADVISER_PITCH.md` Step 3 "Open the entity tree (THE moment)" placeholder populated with entity-tree visual reference + which 3-archetype seeded users best showcase the visual; this row's Up Next entry collapsed to ~~25~~ ✅ SHIPPED; Up Next #26 (Phase 41b) flipped to "UNBLOCKED — next session". `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. Vercel preview will run `prisma migrate deploy` against `monitrax-db-dev` — green build proves the migration applies cleanly + the backfill produces exactly one PERSONAL_NAME entity per existing user.

### 2026-05-04
- **`architect-mode` skill revised — seven lenses + decision-ready synthesis mandate.** Reza explicitly clarified the operating contract: *"I want you to view every change from multiple lenses ... I always need you to give me an informed and consolidated feedback as well. I want you to help with making decisions based on that."* Three changes to `.claude/skills/architect-mode/SKILL.md`: (1) added a 7th lens — **Security & Compliance Consultant** (CDR/threat-model/privacy/credential-egress), explicitly named because Monitrax is CDR-regulated and security is first-class, not folded into "architect"; (2) added a new **Synthesis section** + 6th operating principle (*"Consolidate, don't enumerate"*) — codifies that the seven lenses are internal cognitive work, not user-facing structure; output is a single synthesised recommendation with the lens reasoning compressed into "Why it matters", with explicit anti-patterns ("From the X lens... From the Y lens..." = banned; "here are three options" = banned); (3) tightened the output structure to require a **single Next Best Action** with Implementation specific enough to act on without further clarification. The skill description was updated to reflect the seven lenses + synthesis mandate (970 chars, within 1024 limit). YAML still subordinates skill to CLAUDE.md (CLAUDE.md wins on conflict); skill subordinates to user explicit instructions (user wins on conflict).

- **Phase 36 Phase 2.0 — non-Basiq legacy `/dashboard/accounts` repoint (PR #566)** — `TrailStageIndicator` (Track href), `LinkedDataPanel` (`account` GRDCS add-link), `ModuleHealthBlock` (`accounts` + `offsetAccounts` drill-downs), `app/dashboard/cfo/page.tsx` (Month-End Balance MetricCard `router.push`), `app/api/cashflow/intelligence/route.ts` (Build Emergency Buffer `learnMoreUrl`) all now point at `/dashboard/balances`. Basiq `?action=` hrefs intentionally left in place — they still need the legacy page until Phase 2b ports the Connect Bank UI to Balances. New tech-debt row #9 added to track them.
